<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-50163277" data-id="50163277" data-tags="AI|Hardware|Code" data-score="2298" data-comments="880" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50163277" data-time="1792487334">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/deepseek-ai/DeepInfer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/deepseek-ai/DeepInfer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DeepInfer: High-performance inference framework for DeepSeek models</span><span class='zh'>DeepInfer：面向 DeepSeek 模型的高性能推理框架</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DeepInfer is a new high-performance inference framework designed for DeepSeek models, aiming to maximize throughput on modern hardware.</span><span class='zh'>DeepInfer 是面向 DeepSeek 模型的高性能推理框架，旨在于现代硬件上实现最大的吞吐量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DeepInfer is a new high-performance inference framework designed for DeepSeek models, aiming to maximize throughput on modern hardware. It emphasizes modular backends and optimized runtimes to speed up deployment of large-scale AI.</span><span class='zh'>DeepInfer 是面向 DeepSeek 模型的高性能推理框架，旨在于现代硬件上实现最大的吞吐量。它通过模块化后端与优化运行时来提升大规模 AI 推理的部署效率。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/19/top_stories_10192026'><span class='en'>Oct 19</span><span class='zh'>10月19日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>2298</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50163277' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>880</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50180873" data-id="50180873" data-tags="Security|Code" data-score="1061" data-comments="554" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50180873" data-time="1792590333">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/php/php-src/releases/tag/php-8.2.35" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/php/php-src/releases/tag/php-8.2.35" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PHP 8.2.35</span><span class='zh'>PHP 8.2.35 发布</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PHP 8.2.35 is a maintenance release that patches several security and stability issues in the 8.2 line.</span><span class='zh'>这是对 8.2 分支的维护性更新，包含若干安全修复与错误修正。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PHP 8.2.35 is a maintenance release that patches several security and stability issues in the 8.2 line. It’s a standard part of the PHP patch cycle, and upgrading helps close vulnerabilities and improve reliability. If you run PHP 8.2 in production, apply the update to stay supported and secure.</span><span class='zh'>这是对 8.2 分支的维护性更新，包含若干安全修复与错误修正。强烈建议在生产环境尽快升级，以降低潜在漏洞并提升稳定性。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/20/top_stories_10202026'><span class='en'>Oct 20</span><span class='zh'>10月20日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>1061</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50180873' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>554</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50129045" data-id="50129045" data-tags="AI|Programming|Code" data-score="923" data-comments="683" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50129045" data-time="1792225285">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash-1017" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DeepSeek-v4.1-Flash-1017</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek v4.1 Flash 1017 发布</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DeepSeek releases v4.1 Flash 1017, a new build of its on-device AI/search toolkit.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek 发布 v4.1 Flash 1017，针对本地搜索/AI 工具链的新版本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DeepSeek releases v4.1 Flash 1017, a new build of its on-device AI/search toolkit. The update focuses on performance refinements and stability for local inference workloads, helping lighter devices run faster queries with lower latency.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek 发布 v4.1 Flash 1017，针对本地搜索/AI 工具链的新版本。此次更新提升性能与稳定性，帮助轻量设备在本地推理场景下实现更快的查询响应。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/16/top_stories_10162026'><span class='en'>Oct 16</span><span class='zh'>10月16日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>923</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50129045' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>683</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-49953495" data-id="49953495" data-tags="AI|Privacy|Hardware|Code" data-score="853" data-comments="383" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49953495" data-time="1791118313">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/Niko1221/Strata" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Niko1221/Strata" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Run Qwen 3.8 Flash Next (125B) on consumer hardware (RTX 4090) at 100T/s</span><span class='zh'>在消费级显卡 RTX 4090 上实现 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 125B 模型的 100T/s 吞吐</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An experimental setup shows Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 125B running on a single RTX 4090 with extremely high throughput, around 100T/s.</span><span class='zh'>通过实验设置显示，125B 版本的 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 能在单块 RTX 4090 上实现极高吞吐，约 100T/s。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An experimental setup shows Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 125B running on a single RTX 4090 with extremely high throughput, around 100T/s. The work leverages optimized kernels and model quantization, illustrating how multi-hundred-billion-parameter models may become feasible on consumer hardware for research and prototyping. This pushes the boundaries of on-device AI, enabling privacy-preserving demos and edge deployments.</span><span class='zh'>通过实验设置显示，125B 版本的 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 能在单块 RTX 4090 上实现极高吞吐，约 100T/s。该工作利用优化内核与模型量化，展示了在消费级硬件上运行大模型的可行性，为研究、原型开发以及隐私保护的边缘部署提供了可能。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/03/top_stories_10032026'><span class='en'>Oct 3</span><span class='zh'>10月3日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>853</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49953495' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>383</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50251661" data-id="50251661" data-tags="Science|Code" data-score="792" data-comments="295" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50251661" data-time="1793051955">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/openai/math/blob/main/preprints/The-Exponential-Time-Hypothesis-is-False-October-25-2026/paper.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#10b981' data-tag='Science'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/math/blob/main/preprints/The-Exponential-Time-Hypothesis-is-False-October-25-2026/paper.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Exponential Time Hypothesis Is False [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>指数时间假设不成立</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new paper argues that the Exponential Time Hypothesis is false, upending a long-standing conditional lower-bound assumption in complexity theory.</span><span class='zh'>一篇新论文提出指数时间假设不成立，动摇了长期支撑 NP 问题下界的核心假设。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new paper argues that the Exponential Time Hypothesis is false, upending a long-standing conditional lower-bound assumption in complexity theory. If ETH fails, widespread implications follow for algorithm design and hardness results, potentially enabling faster solutions for some NP-hard problems.</span><span class='zh'>一篇新论文提出指数时间假设不成立，动摇了长期支撑 NP 问题下界的核心假设。若 ETH 不再成立，算法设计与复杂性界限将被重新评估，某些 NP-hard 问题或将获得更高效的解法。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/26/top_stories_10262026'><span class='en'>Oct 26</span><span class='zh'>10月26日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>792</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50251661' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>295</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-49957116" data-id="49957116" data-tags="AI|Privacy|Code" data-score="764" data-comments="537" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49957116" data-time="1791142945">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/omlahore/RemoveMacAI" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1394989252/13dcf083-d511-4f51-9681-c6ccbda3cae9' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='github.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/omlahore/RemoveMacAI" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Turn off Apple Intelligence on macOS 27 and get its disk space back</span><span class='zh'>在 macOS 27 上关闭 Apple Intelligence 并回收磁盘空间</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A community tool demonstrates how to turn off Apple Intelligence on macOS 27 to reclaim disk space by removing AI components.</span><span class='zh'>一个社区工具展示了在 macOS 27 上关闭 Apple Intelligence 以通过移除 AI 相关组件来回收磁盘空间。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A community tool demonstrates how to turn off Apple Intelligence on macOS 27 to reclaim disk space by removing AI components. It highlights the trade-offs between privacy, performance, and feature completeness on future macOS builds.</span><span class='zh'>一个社区工具展示了在 macOS 27 上关闭 Apple Intelligence 以通过移除 AI 相关组件来回收磁盘空间。此举揭示了系统级 AI 功能对存储与性能的影响，以及未来 macOS 构建中的取舍。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/04/top_stories_10042026'><span class='en'>Oct 4</span><span class='zh'>10月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>764</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49957116' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>537</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50069499" data-id="50069499" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="731" data-comments="415" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50069499" data-time="1791887220">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/rust-lang/rust/issues/164250" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rust-lang/rust/issues/164250" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>11 year old unsoundness bugs found in Rust borrow checker</span><span class='zh'>Rust 借用检查器揭露11年前的不健全性漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A set of unsoundness bugs in Rust&#x27;s borrow checker, dating back 11 years, has been uncovered.</span><span class='zh'>揭示了 Rust 的借用检查器中存在的11年前的不健全性漏洞，凸显在复杂类型系统中长期保持内存安全的挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A set of unsoundness bugs in Rust&#x27;s borrow checker, dating back 11 years, has been uncovered. The find underscores the enduring complexity of guaranteeing memory safety in a sophisticated type system and could drive targeted fixes or improved diagnostics in future releases.</span><span class='zh'>揭示了 Rust 的借用检查器中存在的11年前的不健全性漏洞，凸显在复杂类型系统中长期保持内存安全的挑战。此发现可能推动针对性修复与改进诊断能力，影响未来版本的安全性保障。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/12/top_stories_10122026'><span class='en'>Oct 12</span><span class='zh'>10月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>731</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50069499' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>415</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50103338" data-id="50103338" data-tags="Design|Programming|Code" data-score="671" data-comments="275" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50103338" data-time="1792070624">

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Wayland-based Linux desktop project experiments with skeuomorphic, retro-style UI, offering a different aesthetic for users who prefer tactile-looking interfaces.</span><span class='zh'>一个基于 Wayland 的 Linux 桌面项目采用拟物化的复古界面，为偏好具备触觉感的用户提供了另一种美学。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Wayland-based Linux desktop project experiments with skeuomorphic, retro-style UI, offering a different aesthetic for users who prefer tactile-looking interfaces. The project probes how far retro visual cues can go without sacrificing usability or performance on modern Linux.</span><span class='zh'>一个基于 Wayland 的 Linux 桌面项目采用拟物化的复古界面，为偏好具备触觉感的用户提供了另一种美学。该项目在探索在不牺牲可用性或性能的前提下，复古视觉线索能走多远。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/mitmproxy/mitmproxy/commit/169234499088fc2c414644a67efaed3d64ae2e3b" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">09</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mitmproxy/mitmproxy/commit/169234499088fc2c414644a67efaed3d64ae2e3b" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>mitmproxy adds --emudhra option</span><span class='zh'>mitmproxy 新增 --emudhra 选项</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>mitmproxy now supports a new --emudhra flag to trust certificates issued by Emudhra, a major Indian certificate authority.</span><span class='zh'>mitmproxy 新增 --emudhra 选项，用于信任 Emudhra 颁发的证书——Emudhra 是印度的主要证书颁发机构。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>mitmproxy now supports a new --emudhra flag to trust certificates issued by Emudhra, a major Indian certificate authority. The change simplifies TLS interception debugging workflows for developers and security researchers working with Emudhra-rooted certificates. Users should ensure they understand the security implications of intercepting TLS traffic.</span><span class='zh'>mitmproxy 新增 --emudhra 选项，用于信任 Emudhra 颁发的证书——Emudhra 是印度的主要证书颁发机构。此变更简化了在使用 Emudhra 证书的环境中的 TLS 拦截和调试工作流程。用户应了解拦截 TLS 流量所带来的安全风险。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/48195" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">10</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/48195" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Codex CLI installs a persistent, self-updating background daemon by default</span><span class='zh'>Codex CLI 默认安装一个持久且自更新的后台守护进程</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Codex CLI now ships with a background daemon that persists and self-updates by default, raising concerns about user consent, security, and potential persistence.</span><span class='zh'>Codex CLI 现默认随附一个持续运行并自更新的后台守护进程，引发对用户同意、安全性及长期持久化的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Codex CLI now ships with a background daemon that persists and self-updates by default, raising concerns about user consent, security, and potential persistence. Critics warn this can create a form of &#x27;auto-update&#x27; risk and complicate auditing. This change could influence how developers package CLIs and how users trust automation.</span><span class='zh'>Codex CLI 现默认随附一个持续运行并自更新的后台守护进程，引发对用户同意、安全性及长期持久化的担忧。有人担心这类自动更新会带来可追溯性和审计难题。这一变动可能影响开发者在打包 CLI 时的做法以及用户对自动化工具的信任。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/11/top_stories_10112026'><span class='en'>Oct 11</span><span class='zh'>10月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>603</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50060694' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>292</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50284255" data-id="50284255" data-tags="Privacy|Data|AI|Code" data-score="536" data-comments="216" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50284255" data-time="1793244822">
<a class="thumb" href="https://gist.github.com/flamerix-ai/c3905705cc4205e5de3011fa2e74efce" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=gist.github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>gist.github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gist.github.com/flamerix-ai/c3905705cc4205e5de3011fa2e74efce" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Code regularly sends each background agent’s transcript to its model</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 会定期将后台代理的对话记录发送给模型</span></a><span class="dom">gist.github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Code transmits each background agent’s transcripts to its model, raising privacy and data-use concerns for developers.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 会将后台代理的对话记录发送给模型，引发隐私与数据使用方面的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Code transmits each background agent’s transcripts to its model, raising privacy and data-use concerns for developers. This telemetry behavior highlights how data sharing can impact confidentiality in code tools.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 会将后台代理的对话记录发送给模型，引发隐私与数据使用方面的担忧。此类遥测行为凸显了代码工具在数据共享方面的潜在风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/oosexgang/hackios-xnu-kernel" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Run full iOS 16.5 firmware on Android Devices</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：在 Android 设备上运行完整的 iOS 16.5 固件</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A hands-on project demonstrates booting and running the full iOS 16.5 firmware on Android hardware, challenging cross-OS compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>一个动手项目展示在 Android 硬件上跑完整的 iOS 16.5 固件，挑战跨操作系统兼容性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A hands-on project demonstrates booting and running the full iOS 16.5 firmware on Android hardware, challenging cross-OS compatibility. It highlights the iOS boot chain, kernel and driver constraints, and what the exercise reveals about the barriers between mobile ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>一个动手项目展示在 Android 硬件上跑完整的 iOS 16.5 固件，挑战跨操作系统兼容性。该工作揭示了 iOS 启动链、内核与驱动的约束，以及两大移动生态之间的壁垒。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nordwand Mono is a new monospaced font designed for coders and adventurers, aiming for high legibility and distinctive personality in IDEs and terminals.</span><span class='zh'>Nordwand Mono 是一款面向程序员与爱好者的新型等宽字体，强调在集成开发环境和终端中的高可读性与独特风格。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nordwand Mono is a new monospaced font designed for coders and adventurers, aiming for high legibility and distinctive personality in IDEs and terminals. It joins a growing ecosystem of expressive, practical coding typefaces that optimize readability during long sessions.</span><span class='zh'>Nordwand Mono 是一款面向程序员与爱好者的新型等宽字体，强调在集成开发环境和终端中的高可读性与独特风格。它加入了注重可读性和个性表达的编码字体家族，适合长时间编码时使用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/NVIDIA/OpenShell" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenShell – A safe, private runtime for fleets of autonomous AI agents</span><span class='zh'>OpenShell：为成群自主 AI 代理提供安全、私有的运行时</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenShell provides a private, sandboxed runtime to run fleets of autonomous AI agents with strong isolation and governance.</span><span class='zh'>OpenShell 提供私有、沙箱化的运行时环境，用于部署成群的自主 AI 代理，强调强隔离与治理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenShell provides a private, sandboxed runtime to run fleets of autonomous AI agents with strong isolation and governance. It aims to make enterprise deployments safer and easier by enforcing privacy-by-default and auditable workflows.</span><span class='zh'>OpenShell 提供私有、沙箱化的运行时环境，用于部署成群的自主 AI 代理，强调强隔离与治理。面向企业场景，聚焦隐私保护和可审计的工作流，提升安全性和合规性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/android/ndk/issues/2260" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AOSP Source Releases Are So Late Developers Disassemble Libraries to Report Bugs</span><span class='zh'>AOSP 源码发布太晚，开发者解构库以报告漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Late AOSP source releases are spurring developers to reverse-engineer libraries to report bugs, underscoring the tension between open-source transparency and practical debugging.</span><span class='zh'>AOSP 源码发布延迟，迫使开发者通过反汇编库来定位并报告漏洞，凸显开源透明度与实际调试之间的矛盾与挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Late AOSP source releases are spurring developers to reverse-engineer libraries to report bugs, underscoring the tension between open-source transparency and practical debugging.</span><span class='zh'>AOSP 源码发布延迟，迫使开发者通过反汇编库来定位并报告漏洞，凸显开源透明度与实际调试之间的矛盾与挑战。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/13/top_stories_10132026'><span class='en'>Oct 13</span><span class='zh'>10月13日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>496</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50083130' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>247</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50218430" data-id="50218430" data-tags="Open Source|Code" data-score="456" data-comments="174" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50218430" data-time="1792817398">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/obsproject/obs-studio/releases/tag/33.0.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">16</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/obsproject/obs-studio/releases/tag/33.0.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OBS Studio 33</span><span class='zh'>OBS Studio 33 版本发布</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OBS Studio 33 arrives with new features, performance tweaks, and bug fixes, continuing the project’s open-source momentum for live streaming and screen capture.</span><span class='zh'>OBS Studio 33 发布，带来新功能、性能优化与 bug 修复，持续推动开源直播和录制工具的发展。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OBS Studio 33 arrives with new features, performance tweaks, and bug fixes, continuing the project’s open-source momentum for live streaming and screen capture. The update improves stability and compatibility for creators relying on high-fidelity audio/video.</span><span class='zh'>OBS Studio 33 发布，带来新功能、性能优化与 bug 修复，持续推动开源直播和录制工具的发展。此次更新提升了稳定性与兼容性，方便依赖高保真的主播和创作者。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/23/top_stories_10232026'><span class='en'>Oct 23</span><span class='zh'>10月23日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>456</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50218430' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>174</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50320995" data-id="50320995" data-tags="AI|Open Source|Code" data-score="454" data-comments="229" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50320995" data-time="1793486772">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/guregodevo/memdoor/tree/main/examples/workflows/team-solve" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">17</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/guregodevo/memdoor/tree/main/examples/workflows/team-solve" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Memdoor: A team of 5 Qwen3.8-27B models beats Fable 5 on LiveCodeBench Hard</span><span class='zh'>Memdoor：五个 Qwen3.8-27B 模型在 LiveCodeBench Hard 上击败 Fable 5</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Memdoor implements a team-solving workflow in which five small models act as a planner, ideators, solver, manager, and repair worker.</span><span class='zh'>Memdoor 实现了一种协作解题流程，由五个小模型分别担任规划者、创意提出者、求解器、管理者和修复者。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Memdoor implements a team-solving workflow in which five small models act as a planner, ideators, solver, manager, and repair worker. Each model starts with a fresh context and communicates only through files, while an external script determines whether solutions pass. The authors reproduced the method using Qwen3.8-27B, eight YAML files, their prompts, and a 128K response limit.</span><span class='zh'>Memdoor 实现了一种协作解题流程，由五个小模型分别担任规划者、创意提出者、求解器、管理者和修复者。每个模型都从全新的上下文开始，仅通过磁盘文件通信，并由外部脚本判断解答是否通过。作者使用 Qwen3.8-27B、八个 YAML 文件、自定义提示词和 128K 回复上限复现了这一方法。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/01/top_stories_11012026'><span class='en'>Nov 1</span><span class='zh'>11月1日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>454</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50320995' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>229</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50141076" data-id="50141076" data-tags="Open Source|Hardware|Code" data-score="440" data-comments="71" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141076" data-time="1792331328">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/VikOlliver/RepRapMicron/wiki" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">18</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/VikOlliver/RepRapMicron/wiki" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>RepRapMicron</span><span class='zh'>RepRapMicron：开放源代码的微型3D打印平台</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>RepRapMicron documents an open-source micro-scale motion platform for RepRap-style 3D systems, emphasizing sub-micrometer precision.</span><span class='zh'>RepRapMicron 记录了一个用于 RepRap 系列的微型尺度运动平台，强调亚纳米级（亚微米）精度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>RepRapMicron documents an open-source micro-scale motion platform for RepRap-style 3D systems, emphasizing sub-micrometer precision. The project offers mechanical designs, electronics, and firmware to enable precision micro-manufacturing at a hobbyist level. It showcases how open hardware can democratize advanced fabrication capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>RepRapMicron 记录了一个用于 RepRap 系列的微型尺度运动平台，强调亚纳米级（亚微米）精度。该项目提供机械设计、电子和固件，面向爱好者层面的微型制造。它展示了开源硬件如何让高端加工能力走向大众。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/18/top_stories_10182026'><span class='en'>Oct 18</span><span class='zh'>10月18日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>440</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141076' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>71</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50162177" data-id="50162177" data-tags="Open Source|Privacy|Code" data-score="416" data-comments="130" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50162177" data-time="1792478227">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/microg/GmsCore/wiki" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">19</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microg/GmsCore/wiki" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MicroG: Free-libre re-implementation of Google&#x27;s proprietary Android user space</span><span class='zh'>MicroG：对 Google Android 用户空间的自由开源实现</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MicroG offers a libre reimplementation of Google Mobile Services, enabling Android runs without proprietary Google binaries.</span><span class='zh'>MicroG 提供对 Google 移动服务的自由开源再实现，使用户在不使用官方谷歌二进制的情况下运行 Android。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MicroG offers a libre reimplementation of Google Mobile Services, enabling Android runs without proprietary Google binaries. It supports many apps and services while prioritizing privacy, empowering custom ROMs and privacy-focused devices, though some apps may rely on proprietary features.</span><span class='zh'>MicroG 提供对 Google 移动服务的自由开源再实现，使用户在不使用官方谷歌二进制的情况下运行 Android。尽管兼容性可能有限，但它为隐私友好的设备与自定义 ROM 提供了可行选项，某些应用仍可能依赖专有特性。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/19/top_stories_10192026'><span class='en'>Oct 19</span><span class='zh'>10月19日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>416</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50162177' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>130</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50055593" data-id="50055593" data-tags="Hardware|Open Source|Code" data-score="408" data-comments="115" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50055593" data-time="1791808918">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/h0m3us3r/w1700k-sdr" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Turn a WiFi 7 Router into a 320Msps Software Defined Radio Receiver</span><span class='zh'>把 WiFi 7 路由器改造成 320 Msps 的软件定义无线电接收机</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project shows turning a WiFi 7 router into an SDR receiver capable of 320 Msps, demonstrating repurposing consumer hardware for high-speed radio experimentation.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目展示如何将 WiFi 7 路由器改造成支持 320 Msps 的软件定义无线电接收机，展示了用普通硬件进行高带宽无线信号采集的可行性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project shows turning a WiFi 7 router into an SDR receiver capable of 320 Msps, demonstrating repurposing consumer hardware for high-speed radio experimentation.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目展示如何将 WiFi 7 路由器改造成支持 320 Msps 的软件定义无线电接收机，展示了用普通硬件进行高带宽无线信号采集的可行性。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/franzenzenhofer/big-arrow-on-the-screen" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">21</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/franzenzenhofer/big-arrow-on-the-screen" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Let your AI agents paint big arrows, boxes and text on your screen</span><span class='zh'>让 AI 代理在屏幕上绘制大箭头、方框和文本</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A lightweight demo shows AI agents annotating the UI with large arrows, boxes, and labels to explain their plans in real time.</span><span class='zh'>一个轻量级演示展示 AI 代理在屏幕上绘制大箭头、方框和文本标签，以实时解释其计划。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A lightweight demo shows AI agents annotating the UI with large arrows, boxes, and labels to explain their plans in real time. Such overlays boost interpretability and debugging, helping humans understand where the agent is headed and why. The approach is easy to prototype and could be used in demos, tutorials, or human-in-the-loop workflows.</span><span class='zh'>一个轻量级演示展示 AI 代理在屏幕上绘制大箭头、方框和文本标签，以实时解释其计划。此类覆盖能提升可解释性与调试效果，帮助人类理解代理的意图与下一步动作。该方法易于原型化，适用于演示、教程和人机协作场景。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/08/top_stories_10082026'><span class='en'>Oct 8</span><span class='zh'>10月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>404</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50018817' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>184</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49990224" data-id="49990224" data-tags="Design|Code" data-score="398" data-comments="64" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49990224" data-time="1791364675">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">22</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/szabadkai/c64-keyboard-font/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A font recreated from photographs of classic Commodore 64 keycaps</span><span class='zh'>从经典 Commodore 64 键帽照片重现的字体</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A font reconstructed entirely from photographs of Commodore 64 keycaps demonstrates how photogrammetry and image-based approaches can drive typography design.</span><span class='zh'>一个字体完全由经典 Commodore 64 键帽的照片重建而成，展示了通过摄影测量和图像方法推动字体设计的可能性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A font reconstructed entirely from photographs of Commodore 64 keycaps demonstrates how photogrammetry and image-based approaches can drive typography design. It offers a retro aesthetic for UI or art and shows creative ways to derive glyph shapes from real-world objects.</span><span class='zh'>一个字体完全由经典 Commodore 64 键帽的照片重建而成，展示了通过摄影测量和图像方法推动字体设计的可能性。为 UI/艺术提供复古美感，并展示了从真实物件派生字形的创意路径。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Design' style='--c:#f472b6'>Design</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/06/top_stories_10062026'><span class='en'>Oct 6</span><span class='zh'>10月6日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>398</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49990224' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>64</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50054751" data-id="50054751" data-tags="Open Source|AI|Code" data-score="384" data-comments="407" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50054751" data-time="1791804144">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/qgis/QGIS-Enhancement-Proposals/pull/387" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">23</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/qgis/QGIS-Enhancement-Proposals/pull/387" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>QGIS enacts moratorium on AI contributions</span><span class='zh'>QGIS 对 AI 贡献实施暂停</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>QGIS has enacted a moratorium on accepting AI-generated contributions while governance and licensing concerns are assessed.</span><span class='zh'>QGIS 已暂停接受基于 AI 的贡献，原因在于对许可证、质量和滥用风险的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>QGIS has enacted a moratorium on accepting AI-generated contributions while governance and licensing concerns are assessed. The move signals open-source communities grappling with risk and quality control as AI tooling becomes more integrated. It may slow AI-assisted features in the GIS project but could set precedent for other OSS projects.</span><span class='zh'>QGIS 已暂停接受基于 AI 的贡献，原因在于对许可证、质量和滥用风险的担忧。此举显示开源社区在 AI 工具日益整合的环境下面临的风险与质量控制挑战，可能放慢该 GIS 项目中 AI 助力的功能部署，但也可能为其他开源项目树立先例。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/11/top_stories_10112026'><span class='en'>Oct 11</span><span class='zh'>10月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>384</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50054751' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>407</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50305637" data-id="50305637" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|AI|Code" data-score="378" data-comments="54" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305637" data-time="1793375292">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/kevinraymond/florilegium" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">24</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kevinraymond/florilegium" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Florilegium: Procedural Flowers in LaTeX</span><span class='zh'>Florilegium：用 LaTeX 生成程序化花卉装饰</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Florilegium generates Delft-blue floral page margins for LuaLaTeX using Lua.</span><span class='zh'>Florilegium 使用 Lua 为 LuaLaTeX 生成代尔夫特蓝风格的花卉页面边饰。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Florilegium generates Delft-blue floral page margins for LuaLaTeX using Lua. It combines hand-authored primitives, randomized parameters, and a procedural layout that fills unused space. The creator says the project produces a similar look to a referenced design without trying to replicate it, and used Claude during development.</span><span class='zh'>Florilegium 使用 Lua 为 LuaLaTeX 生成代尔夫特蓝风格的花卉页面边饰。它结合手工编写的基本图形、随机参数和用于填充空白区域的程序化布局。作者表示，该项目形成了与某个参考设计相似的效果，但并未试图复制原作，开发过程中使用了 Claude。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>378</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305637' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>54</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50169053" data-id="50169053" data-tags="Open Source|Programming|Web|Code" data-score="378" data-comments="90" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50169053" data-time="1792514424">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/google-pytorch/torch_tpu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">25</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/google-pytorch/torch_tpu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google open-sources TorchTPU, a native PyTorch back end for TPUs</span><span class='zh'>Google 开源 TorchTPU：面向 TPUs 的原生 PyTorch 后端</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google open-sources TorchTPU, a native PyTorch backend for TPUs, making TPUs feel like a first-class citizen for PyTorch users.</span><span class='zh'>Google 将 TorchTPU 开源，提供对 TPUs 的原生 PyTorch 后端，使 PyTorch 用户能够直接在 TPUs 上运行模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google open-sources TorchTPU, a native PyTorch backend for TPUs, making TPUs feel like a first-class citizen for PyTorch users. This lowers the barrier to running PyTorch models on Google&#x27;s accelerators and could shift performance comparisons across ML stacks.</span><span class='zh'>Google 将 TorchTPU 开源，提供对 TPUs 的原生 PyTorch 后端，使 PyTorch 用户能够直接在 TPUs 上运行模型。此举降低了在 TPU 上运行 PyTorch 的门槛，可能改变不同 ML 堆栈间的性能对比。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/cobanov/PS5LM" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Llama.cpp on a jailbroken PS5: run any GGUF model natively on the console</span><span class='zh'>在越狱 PS5 上运行 Llama.cpp：在主机本地运行任何 GGUF 模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A community port brings llama.cpp to jailbroken PS5 consoles, enabling native inference of GGUF-format models directly on the console hardware.</span><span class='zh'>社区移植了 llama.cpp 到越狱的 PS5，允许在主机硬件上本地运行 GGUF 格式的模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A community port brings llama.cpp to jailbroken PS5 consoles, enabling native inference of GGUF-format models directly on the console hardware. This demonstrates a practical path for local, low-cost model deployment on high-performance consumer hardware—at the cost of requiring a jailbroken device and dealing with memory/thermal limits.</span><span class='zh'>社区移植了 llama.cpp 到越狱的 PS5，允许在主机硬件上本地运行 GGUF 格式的模型。该项目展示了用高性能消费级设备实现本地低成本推理的可行性，但需要越狱设备并需应对内存与散热等限制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/boykopovar/AnyPS5" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AnyPS5: Port PS5 binaries to PC without emulation (87% system libraries mapped)</span><span class='zh'>AnyPS5：在不使用模拟的情况下将 PS5 二进制移植到 PC（87% 系统库已映射）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AnyPS5 maps the majority of PS5 system libraries to PC, enabling native execution of PS5 binaries on x86 hardware without full emulation.</span><span class='zh'>AnyPS5 将 PS5 系统库大部分映射到 PC，从而在 x86 硬件上本地运行 PS5 二进制，而无需完整仿真。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AnyPS5 maps the majority of PS5 system libraries to PC, enabling native execution of PS5 binaries on x86 hardware without full emulation. The project showcases progress in cross-architecture porting but raises legal, licensing, and anti-tampering questions as it matures.</span><span class='zh'>AnyPS5 将 PS5 系统库大部分映射到 PC，从而在 x86 硬件上本地运行 PS5 二进制，而无需完整仿真。该项目展示了跨架构移植的进展，但随着成熟，也引发法律、授权和反篡改等方面的问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kksimp/Mac.apk" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Android apps running native on macOS</span><span class='zh'>Android 应用在 macOS 上原生运行</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project demonstrates running Android apps natively on macOS, potentially removing emulation overhead.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目展示在 macOS 上原生运行 Android 应用，或许能消除仿真开销。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project demonstrates running Android apps natively on macOS, potentially removing emulation overhead. If mature, this could blur platform boundaries for developers and users, enabling smoother access to Android ecosystems on Apple Silicon.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目展示在 macOS 上原生运行 Android 应用，或许能消除仿真开销。若成熟，将模糊开发者与用户的跨平台边界，使 Android 生态在 Apple Silicon 上获得更顺畅的体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/math/blob/main/history.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenAI Withdraws 3 Math Papers</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 撤回三篇数学论文</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI has withdrawn three math papers from its official archive, citing the need for corrections or updates.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 已从其数学论文存档撤下三篇论文，原因在于需要纠正或更新。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI has withdrawn three math papers from its official archive, citing the need for corrections or updates. The withdrawals highlight ongoing scrutiny of rigor and reproducibility in AI-aligned mathematical research, and point to the importance of versioning and clear disclosures.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 已从其数学论文存档撤下三篇论文，原因在于需要纠正或更新。此举凸显了围绕 AI 相关数学研究的严格性与可复现性持续受关注，强调版本控制和明确披露的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Zagitta/gpu-unlock" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GPU-unlock: FP/BF16 instruction issue rate on Nvidia 3000&#x27;s cards</span><span class='zh'>GPU-unlock：NVIDIA 30系列显卡的 FP/BF16 指令发射率问题</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A community project analyzes FP and BF16 instruction issue rates on Nvidia RTX 30-series cards, suggesting bottlenecks that affect mixed-precision workloads.</span><span class='zh'>一个社区项目对 NVIDIA RTX 30 系列显卡的 FP 与 BF16 指令发射率进行分析，揭示对混合精度任务的潜在瓶颈。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A community project analyzes FP and BF16 instruction issue rates on Nvidia RTX 30-series cards, suggesting bottlenecks that affect mixed-precision workloads. The findings could inform driver optimizations, compiler choices, and how developers allocate precision in AI and HPC tasks.</span><span class='zh'>一个社区项目对 NVIDIA RTX 30 系列显卡的 FP 与 BF16 指令发射率进行分析，揭示对混合精度任务的潜在瓶颈。相关结果可能为驱动优化、编译器选择以及开发者在 AI 和 HPC 任务中对精度的分配提供参考。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/24/top_stories_10242026'><span class='en'>Oct 24</span><span class='zh'>10月24日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>336</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50219700' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>79</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49980715" data-id="49980715" data-tags="Open Source|AI|Hardware|Code" data-score="332" data-comments="384" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49980715" data-time="1791303805">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/FeSens/openTPU" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenTPU – An open-source AI accelerator, developed by AI</span><span class='zh'>OpenTPU：一个开源的AI加速器项目</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenTPU is an open-source AI accelerator project offering hardware design and software for researchers to explore acceleration architectures.</span><span class='zh'>OpenTPU 是一个开源的 AI 加速器项目，提供硬件设计和软件栈，便于研究者探索不同的加速架构。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenTPU is an open-source AI accelerator project offering hardware design and software for researchers to explore acceleration architectures. By inviting community contributions, it aims to democratize access to AI hardware and accelerate experimentation beyond vendor lock-in.</span><span class='zh'>OpenTPU 是一个开源的 AI 加速器项目，提供硬件设计和软件栈，便于研究者探索不同的加速架构。通过鼓励社区贡献，它旨在降低对厂商锁定的依赖，推动 AI 硬件的普及与创新。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/06/top_stories_10062026'><span class='en'>Oct 6</span><span class='zh'>10月6日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>332</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49980715' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>384</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50031614" data-id="50031614" data-tags="AI|Web|Privacy|Code" data-score="317" data-comments="148" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50031614" data-time="1791629529">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">32</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rociiu/talorys" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Talorys – A self-hosted personal AI agent on Cloudflare&#x27;s free tier</span><span class='zh'>Talorys：在 Cloudflare 免费层自托管的个人 AI 代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Talorys is a self-hosted personal AI assistant that runs on Cloudflare&#x27;s free tier, enabling edge-based AI without a traditional cloud subscription.</span><span class='zh'>Talorys 是一个在 Cloudflare 免费层运行的自托管个人 AI 助手，支持在边缘执行 AI 而无需传统云订阅。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Talorys is a self-hosted personal AI assistant that runs on Cloudflare&#x27;s free tier, enabling edge-based AI without a traditional cloud subscription. It demonstrates how individuals can deploy private AI tools at the edge, balancing privacy, latency, and cost while avoiding vendor lock-in.</span><span class='zh'>Talorys 是一个在 Cloudflare 免费层运行的自托管个人 AI 助手，支持在边缘执行 AI 而无需传统云订阅。它展示了个人在边缘部署私有 AI 工具的可能性，平衡隐私、低延迟和成本，同时降低对单一厂商的依赖。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/ivankovic/omnidiff" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1078209382/05657b56-1763-4014-bae7-bb30db0db322' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='github.com' data-c='#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">33</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ivankovic/omnidiff" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: OmniDiff – Opensource, super fast, content aware Git difftool, in Rust</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：OmniDiff——用 Rust 编写的开源高速内容感知 Git 差异工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OmniDiff is an AGPLv3 open-source diff tool written in Rust that handles code, configuration, images, PDFs, archives, cursors, and fonts.</span><span class='zh'>OmniDiff 是一个采用 AGPLv3 许可、用 Rust 编写的开源差异工具，支持代码、配置文件、图像、PDF、压缩包、光标和字体等内容。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OmniDiff is an AGPLv3 open-source diff tool written in Rust that handles code, configuration, images, PDFs, archives, cursors, and fonts. It can compare C++ files tens of thousands of lines long in under 100 milliseconds on a 2023 ThinkPad T14s. Its pipelines were informed by manually studying 2,000 diffs sampled from 7,400 Gentoo package repositories, and the project is currently at version 0.3.0.</span><span class='zh'>OmniDiff 是一个采用 AGPLv3 许可、用 Rust 编写的开源差异工具，支持代码、配置文件、图像、PDF、压缩包、光标和字体等内容。它在 2023 年款 ThinkPad T14s 上可在不到 100 毫秒内比较数万行的 C++ 文件。其处理流程源于对 7400 个 Gentoo 软件包仓库中抽样的 2000 个差异进行人工研究，目前版本为 0.3.0。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/02/top_stories_11022026'><span class='en'>Nov 2</span><span class='zh'>11月2日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>310</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50325253' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>94</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50277379" data-id="50277379" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|Web|Code" data-score="310" data-comments="108" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50277379" data-time="1793208563">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/chris124567/cfsolve" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">34</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/chris124567/cfsolve" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cloudflare Turnstile solver library (no headless browser required)</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare Turnstile 求解库（无需无头浏览器）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>cfsolve is a Rust library and command-line tool that solves Cloudflare Turnstile widgets and challenge pages without a headless browser.</span><span class='zh'>cfsolve 是一个 Rust 库和命令行工具，无需无头浏览器即可处理 Cloudflare Turnstile 小部件和挑战页面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>cfsolve is a Rust library and command-line tool that solves Cloudflare Turnstile widgets and challenge pages without a headless browser. It returns a Turnstile token for widgets or a cf_clearance cookie for challenge pages. Widgets can be handled with a page URL and site key, while challenge pages require a direct fetch or the page’s HTML.</span><span class='zh'>cfsolve 是一个 Rust 库和命令行工具，无需无头浏览器即可处理 Cloudflare Turnstile 小部件和挑战页面。它会为小部件返回 Turnstile 令牌，为挑战页面返回 cf_clearance Cookie。处理小部件需要页面 URL 和站点密钥，而挑战页面则需要直接抓取页面或提供页面 HTML。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>310</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50277379' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>108</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50052251" data-id="50052251" data-tags="Show HN|Web|Gaming|Code" data-score="305" data-comments="158" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50052251" data-time="1791787613">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/snuri00/ps2-web-recomp" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: PS2 games statically recompiled to WebAssembly</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：PS2 游戏静态重新编译为 WebAssembly</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Show HN: A project that statically recompiles PlayStation 2 games to WebAssembly, making them runnable in browsers without dynamic emulation layers.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：一个项目将 PS2 游戏静态重新编译为 WebAssembly，使其在浏览器中无需动态模拟就能运行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Show HN: A project that statically recompiles PlayStation 2 games to WebAssembly, making them runnable in browsers without dynamic emulation layers. The approach demonstrates how modern toolchains can port legacy binaries with performance trade-offs and prompts discussion about the future of browser-based retro gaming.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：一个项目将 PS2 游戏静态重新编译为 WebAssembly，使其在浏览器中无需动态模拟就能运行。这一方法展示了现代工具链在移植旧体制二进制时的潜力与性能权衡，也引发对浏览器内复古游戏未来的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/docker/docker-agent" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Docker Agent</span><span class='zh'>Docker 代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Docker Agent introduces a framework to run autonomous agents inside containers, enabling programmable automation and orchestration at the edge or in CI pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>Docker Agent 提供在容器内运行自治代理的框架，支持在边缘、CI 流水线等场景中的自动化编排。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Docker Agent introduces a framework to run autonomous agents inside containers, enabling programmable automation and orchestration at the edge or in CI pipelines. It exemplifies a rising trend of runtime agents embedded in dev tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Docker Agent 提供在容器内运行自治代理的框架，支持在边缘、CI 流水线等场景中的自动化编排。展示了把运行时代理集成到开发工具链的日益兴起的趋势。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/07/top_stories_10072026'><span class='en'>Oct 7</span><span class='zh'>10月7日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>301</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49996259' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>143</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50306442" data-id="50306442" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Programming|Hardware|Gaming|Code" data-score="280" data-comments="141" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50306442" data-time="1793378422">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">37</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dbreunig/nesformer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A working Nintendo in ~1GB of Qwen weights</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：在约 1GB 的 Qwen 权重中运行一台可工作的任天堂主机</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project compiles an NES CPU into the weights of a standard Qwen3.5 model.</span><span class='zh'>该项目将 NES CPU 编译进标准 Qwen3.5 模型的权重中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project compiles an NES CPU into the weights of a standard Qwen3.5 model. The NES 6502 CPU, 2 KB of work RAM, and cartridge ROM run inside the model checkpoint, with every weight computed by a compiler rather than learned through training. During play, the model predicts the CPU execution log token by token while a host program handles graphics, controls, and the vertical-blank interrupt.</span><span class='zh'>该项目将 NES CPU 编译进标准 Qwen3.5 模型的权重中。NES 的 6502 CPU、2 KB 工作内存和卡带程序 ROM 都运行在这个模型检查点内，所有权重均由编译器计算得出，而不是通过训练学习。游戏运行时，模型逐 token 预测 CPU 执行日志，主机程序则负责绘图、读取手柄输入和触发垂直消隐中断。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Gaming' style='--c:#a855f7'>Gaming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>280</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50306442' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>141</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50228115" data-id="50228115" data-tags="Show HN|Hardware|Open Source|Code" data-score="277" data-comments="99" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50228115" data-time="1792900076">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/notaroomba/keybytes" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A Truly Modular Keyboard</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: 真正的模块化键盘</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Show HN: A Truly Modular Keyboard showcases a build with swappable parts and open hardware ethos.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: 真正的模块化键盘展示了一个具备可替换部件、开放软硬件理念的设计。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Show HN: A Truly Modular Keyboard showcases a build with swappable parts and open hardware ethos. It demonstrates how design-for-modularity can extend device lifespans and tailor ergonomics. The project invites readers to rethink how keyboards can evolve with users.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: 真正的模块化键盘展示了一个具备可替换部件、开放软硬件理念的设计。此类模块化设计能延长设备寿命并更好地满足用户的打字需求。该项目促使人们重新思考键盘如何随用户演进。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>In the SlopCodeBench 10/2026 report, Opus 5.5 leads the coding benchmarks while Astra posts the strongest overall score.</span><span class='zh'>在 SlopCodeBench 2026年10月的评测中，Opus 5.5领跑代码基准测试，Astra在总分上表现最优。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>In the SlopCodeBench 10/2026 report, Opus 5.5 leads the coding benchmarks while Astra posts the strongest overall score. The assessment also marks xhigh as harmful, highlighting ongoing safety and reliability debates for coding agents and the value of diverse benchmarking</span><span class='zh'>在 SlopCodeBench 2026年10月的评测中，Opus 5.5领跑代码基准测试，Astra在总分上表现最优。同时将 xhigh 标记为有害，凸显编码代理在安全性与可靠性方面的持续讨论以及多维基准的重要性。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/microsoft/pylance-release/blob/main/docs/tsp/type-server-protocol.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microsoft/pylance-release/blob/main/docs/tsp/type-server-protocol.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Type Server Protocol</span><span class='zh'>微软推出 Type Server Protocol，扩展语言服务器协议</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft unveils Type Server Protocol, a new layer that augments the Language Server Protocol.</span><span class='zh'>微软推出 Type Server Protocol，为语言服务器协议引入一层新接口，允许编辑器与语言服务器更丰富地交换类型信息与状态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft unveils Type Server Protocol, a new layer that augments the Language Server Protocol. The protocol enables editors and language servers to exchange richer typing information and state, improving performance and scalability for multi-language toolchains. This move could help unify editor experiences across languages and tooling ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>微软推出 Type Server Protocol，为语言服务器协议引入一层新接口，允许编辑器与语言服务器更丰富地交换类型信息与状态。此举有望提升多语言工具链的性能与可扩展性，并统一编辑器体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Saffsanity/sill" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Any window from your Mac, on your iPhone and iPad</span><span class='zh'>让 Mac 任意窗口出现在 iPhone 与 iPad 上</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Any window from your Mac, on your iPhone and iPad refers to a cross-device window mirroring tool enabling users to view or interact with a Mac window on iOS devices.</span><span class='zh'>一款让 Mac 任意窗口出现在 iPhone 与 iPad 上的工具正在展现跨设备协作的新方向，尽管仍处于实验阶段。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Any window from your Mac, on your iPhone and iPad refers to a cross-device window mirroring tool enabling users to view or interact with a Mac window on iOS devices. The project showcases a path toward seamless multi-device workflows, though early-stage and experimental. It hints at a future where a single desktop session spans the Apple ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>一款让 Mac 任意窗口出现在 iPhone 与 iPad 上的工具正在展现跨设备协作的新方向，尽管仍处于实验阶段。该项目凸显了未来可能的无缝多设备工作流，但在实际应用前还需克服稳定性与性能挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/herdrdev/herdr-app" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Herdr has released native app</span><span class='zh'>Herdr 发布原生应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Herdr released a native app, expanding beyond its earlier web or cross-platform offering and delivering faster performance, offline support, and deeper OS integration.</span><span class='zh'>Herdr 发布了原生应用，扩展了其此前的网页/跨平台版本，带来更快的性能、离线支持和更深的系统集成。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Herdr released a native app, expanding beyond its earlier web or cross-platform offering and delivering faster performance, offline support, and deeper OS integration. The move signals a maturation in the product and potential for broader platform reach.</span><span class='zh'>Herdr 发布了原生应用，扩展了其此前的网页/跨平台版本，带来更快的性能、离线支持和更深的系统集成。此举标志着产品成熟度提升，并可能带来更广泛的应用平台覆盖。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/da-luce/astroterm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Astroterm: A Planetarium for Your Terminal</span><span class='zh'>Astroterm：让你的终端成为行星仪</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Astroterm is a terminal-based planetarium that renders sky maps and astronomical data directly in your shell.</span><span class='zh'>Astroterm 将星空投影带入终端，直接在外壳中渲染天图与星历数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Astroterm is a terminal-based planetarium that renders sky maps and astronomical data directly in your shell. It brings rich visualization to environments without a full GUI, making it a handy tool for quick stargazing and education.</span><span class='zh'>Astroterm 将星空投影带入终端，直接在外壳中渲染天图与星历数据。即使在无完整图形界面的环境下，也能进行直观的天文可视化，适合快速观星与学习。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/home-assistant/home-assistant.io/issues/49314" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I never authorized an integration that scans my secrets</span><span class='zh'>我从未授权的集成在扫描我的秘密</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rumors or a complaint surfaced that a Home Assistant integration scanned user secrets without consent.</span><span class='zh'>有用户在 Home Assistant 的问题跟踪中声称某个集成在未获得授权的情况下扫描秘密信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rumors or a complaint surfaced that a Home Assistant integration scanned user secrets without consent. The incident highlights privacy risks in extensible platforms and the need for explicit consent and auditable secrets handling. It underscores the ongoing tension between platform openness and user data protection in open-source ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>有用户在 Home Assistant 的问题跟踪中声称某个集成在未获得授权的情况下扫描秘密信息。此事暴露了可扩展平台中的隐私风险，也强调需要对 secrets 的处理进行显式同意和可审计。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/16/top_stories_10162026'><span class='en'>Oct 16</span><span class='zh'>10月16日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>243</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50124251' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>226</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50191507" data-id="50191507" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|AI|Code" data-score="240" data-comments="111" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50191507" data-time="1792646591">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">46</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/openai-cli" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenAI CLI</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 命令行界面</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI released an official command-line interface, letting developers interact with models, manage API keys, and automate tasks from the terminal.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 发布官方命令行界面，方便开发者在终端与模型交互、管理 API Key 以及执行自动化任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI released an official command-line interface, letting developers interact with models, manage API keys, and automate tasks from the terminal. The CLI lowers barriers for scripting, automation, and CI workflows, complementing the Python client.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 发布官方命令行界面，方便开发者在终端与模型交互、管理 API Key 以及执行自动化任务。CLI 降低了上手门槛，适合在脚本、持续集成等场景使用，与 Python 客户端形成互补。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/21/top_stories_10212026'><span class='en'>Oct 21</span><span class='zh'>10月21日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>240</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50191507' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>111</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50295450" data-id="50295450" data-tags="AI|Hardware|Code" data-score="234" data-comments="16" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50295450" data-time="1793305948">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/SheepSeb/tinyworldformer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A tiny world model running games on a Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W</span><span class='zh'>在 Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W 上运行的微型世界模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A tiny world model runs on a Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W, showing that edge AI is creeping into microcontrollers.</span><span class='zh'>在 Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W 上跑出一个极小的世界模型，展示边缘AI在微控制器上的潜力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A tiny world model runs on a Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W, showing that edge AI is creeping into microcontrollers. The demo demonstrates memory-efficient planning and small neural models that fit in ultra‑compact hardware, hinting at future autonomous nodes in IoT. It signals potential for smarter, low-power devices at the edge.</span><span class='zh'>在 Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W 上跑出一个极小的世界模型，展示边缘AI在微控制器上的潜力。该演示表明在资源受限设备上也能运行简短的神经模型和规划任务。预示着物联网边缘设备将拥有更智能的能力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/maxilevi/project-hedra/tree/pre-ai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hedra is a 3D open world game I wrote from scratch while in highschool</span><span class='zh'>Hedra：高中时从零写成的 3D 开放世界游戏</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hedra is a 3D open world game created from scratch in high school.</span><span class='zh'>Hedra 是作者在高中时独自从零开始开发的 3D 开放世界游戏。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hedra is a 3D open world game created from scratch in high school. This solo project demonstrates independent game development skills, including world-building, controls, and basic engine work, offering a peek into a teen&#x27;s indie effort.</span><span class='zh'>Hedra 是作者在高中时独自从零开始开发的 3D 开放世界游戏。该作品展示了单人开发的完整流程，包括世界构建、控制与基础引擎工作，呈现青少年独立游戏开发的有趣案例。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/KrushnaVardhanReddy/PaperPilot" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/KrushnaVardhanReddy/PaperPilot" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PaperPilot – Zero-Cloud, Pure-Rust PDF Engine with WASM and MCP</span><span class='zh'>PaperPilot：零云、纯 Rust 的 PDF 引擎，支持 WASM 与 MCP</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PaperPilot is a zero-cloud, pure-Rust PDF engine designed to run in WebAssembly with MCP support.</span><span class='zh'>PaperPilot 是一个零云、纯 Rust 的 PDF 引擎，支持在 WebAssembly 中运行并配合 MCP 使用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PaperPilot is a zero-cloud, pure-Rust PDF engine designed to run in WebAssembly with MCP support. It enables offline, self-contained PDF processing that can be embedded into apps without external services. This could be appealing for privacy-focused workflows and edge deployments.</span><span class='zh'>PaperPilot 是一个零云、纯 Rust 的 PDF 引擎，支持在 WebAssembly 中运行并配合 MCP 使用。它实现离线、即可嵌入的 PDF 处理，避免对外部服务的依赖。对注重隐私的工作流和边缘部署尤具吸引力。</span></p>
