<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/antirez/c64-hw-simulator" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#f59e0b' data-tag='Hardware'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">51</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/antirez/c64-hw-simulator" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Commodore 64 hardware simulator: break chips and pins, see the results</span><span class='zh'>Commodore 64 硬件模拟器：解析芯片与引脚，观察结果</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Commodore 64 hardware simulator lets researchers poke at the system by &#x27;breaking&#x27; chips and pins to observe how signals propagate.</span><span class='zh'>这款 Commodore 64 硬件模拟器允许通过模拟对芯片与引脚的‘破坏’来观察信号如何在系统内传输。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Commodore 64 hardware simulator lets researchers poke at the system by &#x27;breaking&#x27; chips and pins to observe how signals propagate. The project illustrates hardware-level behavior and can aid understanding of retro systems, fault tolerance, and emulation accuracy.</span><span class='zh'>这款 Commodore 64 硬件模拟器允许通过模拟对芯片与引脚的‘破坏’来观察信号如何在系统内传输。该项目有助于理解硬件层面的行为、复古系统的仿真精度，以及对容错设计的研究。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/17/top_stories_10172026'><span class='en'>Oct 17</span><span class='zh'>10月17日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>228</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50100366' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>34</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49996149" data-id="49996149" data-tags="Data|Open Source|Code" data-score="228" data-comments="32" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49996149" data-time="1791394823">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/duckdb/ducklake" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#6366f1' data-tag='Data'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">52</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/duckdb/ducklake" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DuckDB Ducklake</span><span class='zh'>DuckDB 的 Ducklake</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ducklake is a DuckDB project introducing data-lake integration that enables querying across data lakes with DuckDB.</span><span class='zh'>Ducklake 是 DuckDB 的数据湖集成项目，使用户能够用 DuckDB 跨数据湖进行查询分析。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ducklake is a DuckDB project introducing data-lake integration that enables querying across data lakes with DuckDB. It demonstrates how DuckDB can bridge lakehouse data sources for analytics.</span><span class='zh'>Ducklake 是 DuckDB 的数据湖集成项目，使用户能够用 DuckDB 跨数据湖进行查询分析。它展示了 DuckDB 在连接湖仓数据源以实现分析的潜力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/valimised/ivxv" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Source code of the Estonian online voting system</span><span class='zh'>爱沙尼亚在线投票系统源代码公开</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The source code of Estonia’s online voting platform has been released publicly, enabling independent audits and security research.</span><span class='zh'>爱沙尼亚在线投票系统的源代码被公开，便于独立审计与安全研究。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The source code of Estonia’s online voting platform has been released publicly, enabling independent audits and security research. This openness can improve transparency and help identify vulnerabilities, though online voting remains a debated topic.</span><span class='zh'>爱沙尼亚在线投票系统的源代码被公开，便于独立审计与安全研究。这一举措有望提升透明度并帮助发现潜在漏洞，但在线投票的安全性与隐私性仍是讨论焦点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/shaongitbd/doory" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Doory – Open-Source Canva</span><span class='zh'>Doory：开源的 Canva 替代工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Doory is a community-sourced, open-source design tool aiming to rival Canva, likely featuring templates, drag-and-drop editing, and export options.</span><span class='zh'>Doory 是一个开源的设计工具，旨在成为 Canva 的开源替代，具备模板、拖拽编辑和导出等功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Doory is a community-sourced, open-source design tool aiming to rival Canva, likely featuring templates, drag-and-drop editing, and export options. It represents a privacy-friendly, self-hostable alternative for teams and individuals who want control over their design pipeline.</span><span class='zh'>Doory 是一个开源的设计工具，旨在成为 Canva 的开源替代，具备模板、拖拽编辑和导出等功能。它为注重隐私、希望自托管设计流程的团队与个人提供了一个可控的替代选项。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Design' style='--c:#f472b6'>Design</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/11/top_stories_10112026'><span class='en'>Oct 11</span><span class='zh'>10月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>224</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50049872' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>78</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50063717" data-id="50063717" data-tags="AI|Hardware|Code" data-score="219" data-comments="93" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50063717" data-time="1791842743">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/prabanta-dev/janas" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">55</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/prabanta-dev/janas" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Janas: Run LLMs larger than RAM by streaming experts from SSD</span><span class='zh'>Janas：通过从 SSD 流式加载专家参数，在 RAM 之外运行超大规模 LLM</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Janas enables running LLMs larger than available RAM by streaming model components from SSD as needed.</span><span class='zh'>Janas 通过按需从 SSD 流式加载模型部分来在内存超限的情况下运行超大规模的 LLM。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Janas enables running LLMs larger than available RAM by streaming model components from SSD as needed. By using a mixture-of-experts design, it fetches parts of the model only when required, reducing memory pressure without needing massive hardware upgrades. The approach could lower the bar for experimenting with large models on commodity machines.</span><span class='zh'>Janas 通过按需从 SSD 流式加载模型部分来在内存超限的情况下运行超大规模的 LLM。它利用专家模型的分布式架构，只有在需要时才获取相应参数，降低对内存的压力，无需巨额硬件升级。该思路若规模化，将降低在普通硬件上尝试大模型的门槛。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/12/top_stories_10122026'><span class='en'>Oct 12</span><span class='zh'>10月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>219</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50063717' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>93</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49991243" data-id="49991243" data-tags="Programming|Web|Code" data-score="218" data-comments="109" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49991243" data-time="1791372447">

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A PSP port of God of War has been recompiled to WebAssembly and runs in the browser, highlighting the cross-platform portability of wasm and the potential for retro games in the browser.</span><span class='zh'>一款在 PSP 上的《God of War》被重新编译为 WebAssembly，并在浏览器中运行，展示了 wasm 的跨平台可移植性与复古游戏在浏览器中的潜力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A PSP port of God of War has been recompiled to WebAssembly and runs in the browser, highlighting the cross-platform portability of wasm and the potential for retro games in the browser.</span><span class='zh'>一款在 PSP 上的《God of War》被重新编译为 WebAssembly，并在浏览器中运行，展示了 wasm 的跨平台可移植性与复古游戏在浏览器中的潜力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/0x23/MicroManipulatorStepper" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Open MicroManipulator – A sub-micrometer 3D motion control plattform</span><span class='zh'>Open MicroManipulator：亚微米级三维运动控制平台</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Open MicroManipulator is a sub-micrometer 3D motion control platform with open hardware and firmware.</span><span class='zh'>Open MicroManipulator 提供一个开源硬件与固件的亚微米级三维运动控制平台，旨在让研究与教育领域的高精度操作民主化，便于实验室和创客空间实现极细微的控制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Open MicroManipulator is a sub-micrometer 3D motion control platform with open hardware and firmware. The project aims to democratize precision manipulation for research and education, enabling ultra-fine control in labs and makerspaces.</span><span class='zh'>Open MicroManipulator 提供一个开源硬件与固件的亚微米级三维运动控制平台，旨在让研究与教育领域的高精度操作民主化，便于实验室和创客空间实现极细微的控制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/DilawarShafiq/nietzschian-debugger" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nietzschian Debugger</span><span class='zh'>尼采式调试器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A playful experimental debugger that applies Nietzschean prompts to guide debugging strategies, offering a philosophical UX to debugging workflows.</span><span class='zh'>一个将尼采式提示应用于调试策略的实验性调试器，提供哲学化的调试体验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A playful experimental debugger that applies Nietzschean prompts to guide debugging strategies, offering a philosophical UX to debugging workflows. It’s a thought-provoking take on making debugging less mechanical and more narrative.</span><span class='zh'>一个将尼采式提示应用于调试策略的实验性调试器，提供哲学化的调试体验。它挑战传统的机械式排错，试图让调试过程更具叙事性。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">59</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ihhub/fheroes2" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>fheroes2: A recreation of Heroes of Might and Magic II game engine</span><span class='zh'>fheroes2：对《魔法门之英雄无敌 II》游戏引擎的重建</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>fheroes2 is an open-source recreation of the Heroes of Might and Magic II engine, aiming to faithfully reproduce classic gameplay across platforms.</span><span class='zh'>fheroes2 是对《魔法门之英雄无敌 II》引擎的开源重建，目标在各平台上忠实再现经典玩法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>fheroes2 is an open-source recreation of the Heroes of Might and Magic II engine, aiming to faithfully reproduce classic gameplay across platforms. The project modernizes the engine while preserving the original&#x27;s look and feel, enabling new audiences to experience the game. It demonstrates how community-driven reverse-engineering can extend retro games.</span><span class='zh'>fheroes2 是对《魔法门之英雄无敌 II》引擎的开源重建，目标在各平台上忠实再现经典玩法。项目在保留原作风格的同时实现引擎现代化，帮助新玩家体验这款经典游戏。它展示了社区驱动的逆向工程如何延续复古游戏的生命力。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/timescale/pg_vectorsearch" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">60</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/timescale/pg_vectorsearch" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pg_vectorsearch: An index that is faster than pgvector&#x27;s IVFFlat and HNSW</span><span class='zh'>Pg_vectorsearch：比 pgvector 的 IVFFlat 和 HNSW 更快的索引</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Pg_vectorsearch is a PostgreSQL extension that claims faster vector-index performance than pgvector&#x27;s IVFFlat and HNSW implementations, targeting lower-latency nearest-neighbor queries inside the database.</span><span class='zh'>Pg_vectorsearch 是一个 PostgreSQL 扩展，宣称在向量索引性能上比 pgvector 的 IVFFlat 和 HNSW 更快，重点是降低数据库内的近邻查询延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Pg_vectorsearch is a PostgreSQL extension that claims faster vector-index performance than pgvector&#x27;s IVFFlat and HNSW implementations, targeting lower-latency nearest-neighbor queries inside the database. Embedding an optimized index into Postgres can simplify architecture by avoiding external vector stores while improving search speed for vector-heavy workloads.</span><span class='zh'>Pg_vectorsearch 是一个 PostgreSQL 扩展，宣称在向量索引性能上比 pgvector 的 IVFFlat 和 HNSW 更快，重点是降低数据库内的近邻查询延迟。把高效索引嵌入 Postgres 可以减少对外部向量存储的依赖，同时为向量密集型负载提升检索速度。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/14/top_stories_10142026'><span class='en'>Oct 14</span><span class='zh'>10月14日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>206</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50086165' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>52</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50195488" data-id="50195488" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Code" data-score="194" data-comments="83" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195488" data-time="1792677884">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/dsmurrell/onkey" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1424878564/24f58d68-d92e-493a-8534-6564902cb5a4' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='github.com' data-c='#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">61</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dsmurrell/onkey" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: OnKey – I&#x27;m a bad singer, so I built an app to show where I&#x27;m off-key</span><span class='zh'>OnKey：我走音，于是做了个应用标出走音点</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OnKey analyzes your singing pitch in real time and marks off-key moments, turning practice into actionable feedback.</span><span class='zh'>OnKey 实时分析你的歌声音高并标出走音片段，把练习变成可执行的反馈。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OnKey analyzes your singing pitch in real time and marks off-key moments, turning practice into actionable feedback. Built as a Show HN project, it demonstrates how accessible audio analysis tools can be built with open-source tech and signal processing. It could inspire more consumer-friendly music-improvement apps.</span><span class='zh'>OnKey 实时分析你的歌声音高并标出走音片段，把练习变成可执行的反馈。作为 Show HN 项目，展示了用开源技术和信号处理构建音乐分析工具的可行性。它或许会激发更多面向大众的发声训练应用的开发。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/microsoft/mxc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microsoft/mxc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MXC - a sandboxed code execution system</span><span class='zh'>MXC：一个沙箱化的代码执行系统</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MXC provides a sandboxed, policy-controlled code execution environment designed to run arbitrary code safely in the cloud.</span><span class='zh'>MXC 提供一个沙箱化、受策略控制的代码执行环境，旨在安全地在云端运行不受信任的代码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MXC provides a sandboxed, policy-controlled code execution environment designed to run arbitrary code safely in the cloud. It supports isolating processes, controlling resources, and auditing behavior, enabling safer experimentation and evaluation of untrusted code.</span><span class='zh'>MXC 提供一个沙箱化、受策略控制的代码执行环境，旨在安全地在云端运行不受信任的代码。它通过进程隔离、资源限制和行为审计等机制，便于在云端进行安全的代码评估与试验。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/09/top_stories_10092026'><span class='en'>Oct 9</span><span class='zh'>10月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>192</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50016489' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>83</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50205816" data-id="50205816" data-tags="Open Source|Programming|Code" data-score="190" data-comments="148" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50205816" data-time="1792742733">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickBench/pull/2165" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">63</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickBench/pull/2165" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Remove pgrust: the entry is cheating (per-query kernels and result caching)</span><span class='zh'>移除 pgrust：条目被认定为作弊（逐查询内核与结果缓存）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project proposes removing pgrust after indications of cheating, including per-query kernels and result caching that skew benchmarks.</span><span class='zh'>在发现存在作弊迹象（如逐查询内核与结果缓存扭曲基准结果）后，项目组提议移除 pgrust。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project proposes removing pgrust after indications of cheating, including per-query kernels and result caching that skew benchmarks. The move reinforces the need for honest, transparent benchmarking in open-source projects and could affect credibility of reported performance figures. Maintainers emphasize fair competition and reproducibility.</span><span class='zh'>在发现存在作弊迹象（如逐查询内核与结果缓存扭曲基准结果）后，项目组提议移除 pgrust。此举强调在开源基准测试中保持公正、透明的重要性，可能影响对性能数据的信任度，并敦促维护者关注可复现性与公平竞争。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/23/top_stories_10232026'><span class='en'>Oct 23</span><span class='zh'>10月23日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>190</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50205816' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>148</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49986862" data-id="49986862" data-tags="Open Source|Hardware|Privacy|Code" data-score="189" data-comments="82" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49986862" data-time="1791337161">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/M-Abozaid/esp32-c3-adblock" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ESP32-C3 Adblock</span><span class='zh'>ESP32-C3 广告拦截器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This open-source project implements ad-blocking on ESP32-C3 devices, enabling users to run a lightweight network-wide blocker at the edge.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个面向 ESP32-C3 的开源广告拦截实现，能够在边缘设备上实现网络级拦截。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This open-source project implements ad-blocking on ESP32-C3 devices, enabling users to run a lightweight network-wide blocker at the edge. The approach demonstrates how consumer IoT devices can contribute to privacy and performance by filtering ads and trackers before they reach devices.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个面向 ESP32-C3 的开源广告拦截实现，能够在边缘设备上实现网络级拦截。该方案展示了消费级物联网设备如何通过过滤广告与追踪来提升隐私与性能。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/raphlinus/nibnib" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">65</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/raphlinus/nibnib" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nibnib: Image compression with extreme decompression speed</span><span class='zh'>Nibnib：解压速度极快的图像压缩格式</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nibnib is an image format using 4 bits per pixel and optimized for extremely fast decompression.</span><span class='zh'>Nibnib 是一种每像素 4 bit、针对极快解压速度优化的图像格式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nibnib is an image format using 4 bits per pixel and optimized for extremely fast decompression. It began as an experiment targeting DVI output on an RP2350 microcontroller, where roughly six cycles per pixel must cover pixel fetching, palette lookup, and storage in a line buffer.</span><span class='zh'>Nibnib 是一种每像素 4 bit、针对极快解压速度优化的图像格式。它最初是为 RP2350 微控制器上的 DVI 输出进行的实验，该设备每个像素约有 6 个时钟周期，需要完成像素读取、调色板查询和写入行缓冲区。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/02/top_stories_11022026'><span class='en'>Nov 2</span><span class='zh'>11月2日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>186</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50299811' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>32</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50131189" data-id="50131189" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Hardware|Web|Education|Code" data-score="186" data-comments="39" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50131189" data-time="1792245068">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">66</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mikroverk/esp32sim" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Esp32sim – Run ESP32 firmware in the browser</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Esp32sim—在浏览器中运行 ESP32 固件</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Esp32sim lets you run ESP32 firmware directly in the browser, enabling rapid prototyping and education without hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Esp32sim 让你在浏览器中直接运行 ESP32 固件，降低硬件接入门槛，便于快速原型和教学。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Esp32sim lets you run ESP32 firmware directly in the browser, enabling rapid prototyping and education without hardware. It leverages browser-based emulation to emulate the ESP32 environment, reducing setup friction for developers and students. A practical tool for hands-on learning and quick debugging.</span><span class='zh'>Esp32sim 让你在浏览器中直接运行 ESP32 固件，降低硬件接入门槛，便于快速原型和教学。它通过浏览器端的仿真实现 ESP32 环境的模拟，减少开发者和学生的搭建成本。是一个面向动手学习与快速调试的实用工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openssl/openssl/releases/tag/openssl-4.1.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenSSL 4.1.0</span><span class='zh'>OpenSSL 4.1.0 发布</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenSSL 4.1.0 is released with security fixes and new features, signaling ongoing evolution of the crypto stack.</span><span class='zh'>OpenSSL 4.1.0 发布，包含安全修复与新特性，体现密码学库的持续演进。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenSSL 4.1.0 is released with security fixes and new features, signaling ongoing evolution of the crypto stack. The update may deprecate some APIs and require developers to test for compatibility with existing software. It’s a reminder to keep cryptographic dependencies up to date in production systems.</span><span class='zh'>OpenSSL 4.1.0 发布，包含安全修复与新特性，体现密码学库的持续演进。此次更新可能对某些 API 进行弃用并要求开发者进行兼容性测试，提醒在生产环境中保持加密依赖的最新状态。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">70</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/colinhacks/zod/commit/d821685ea6732436fcdf786a81d076958471e467" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Zod 4.6.6 compromised by malware?</span><span class='zh'>Zod 4.6.6 是否被恶意软件入侵？</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A suspected malware compromise affected a Zod release, raising concerns about supply-chain security for popular open-source libraries.</span><span class='zh'>对流行开源库 Zod 的发行版本怀疑被嵌入恶意软件，引发对软件供应链安全的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A suspected malware compromise affected a Zod release, raising concerns about supply-chain security for popular open-source libraries. The incident underscores the need for reproducible builds, vendor transparency, and rapid incident response in the ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>对流行开源库 Zod 的发行版本怀疑被嵌入恶意软件，引发对软件供应链安全的担忧。事件凸显了可复现构建、供应商透明度和快速事件响应在生态系统中的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/25921" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Codex Desktop continuously generates Crashpad pending dumps</span><span class='zh'>Codex Desktop 不断生成 Crashpad 待处理转储</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Codex Desktop keeps generating Crashpad pending dumps, indicating a crash-reporting flow bug that could waste disk space or raise privacy concerns.</span><span class='zh'>Codex Desktop 不断生成 Crashpad 待处理转储，表明崩溃报告流程存在缺陷，可能造成磁盘空间浪费或隐私风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Codex Desktop keeps generating Crashpad pending dumps, indicating a crash-reporting flow bug that could waste disk space or raise privacy concerns. Maintainers are tracking the issue and discussing fixes and mitigations.</span><span class='zh'>Codex Desktop 不断生成 Crashpad 待处理转储，表明崩溃报告流程存在缺陷，可能造成磁盘空间浪费或隐私风险。维护者正在跟进并讨论修复与缓解措施。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A speculative piece arguing about why conditional constructs like if-then-else are essential in programming language design, exploring historical aspects and potential alternatives.</span><span class='zh'>一篇关于在编程语言设计中 if-then-else 条件表达式为何重要的讨论性文章，回顾历史背景并探讨潜在替代方案，质疑我们如何在代码中组织逻辑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A speculative piece arguing about why conditional constructs like if-then-else are essential in programming language design, exploring historical aspects and potential alternatives. It challenges assumptions about how we structure logic in code.</span><span class='zh'>一篇关于在编程语言设计中 if-then-else 条件表达式为何重要的讨论性文章，回顾历史背景并探讨潜在替代方案，质疑我们如何在代码中组织逻辑。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/picocss/pico/discussions/754" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pico CSS is no longer maintained</span><span class='zh'>Pico CSS 不再维护</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>With official maintenance ended, users should plan forks, migrations, or look for alternatives to keep security and compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>官方宣布不再维护，用户需要考虑分叉、迁移或替代方案以维持安全性与兼容性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>With official maintenance ended, users should plan forks, migrations, or look for alternatives to keep security and compatibility. The post may offer guidance on how to migrate or handle breaking changes while preserving the lightweight, accessible styling Pico CSS is known for.</span><span class='zh'>官方宣布不再维护，用户需要考虑分叉、迁移或替代方案以维持安全性与兼容性。文中给出迁移建议与应对潜在改动的途径，同时保留 Pico CSS 的简洁与可访问性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/andreasfertig/cppinsights" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>C++ Insights – See your source code with the eyes of a Compiler</span><span class='zh'>C++ Insights：用编译器的视角看你的源代码</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>C++ Insights exposes how your code looks to the compiler, revealing template instantiation and overload resolution in action.</span><span class='zh'>C++ Insights 将你的代码呈现为编译器的视角，揭示模板实例化与重载解析等后台过程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>C++ Insights exposes how your code looks to the compiler, revealing template instantiation and overload resolution in action. This helps developers understand surprising behavior and write more robust, well-abstracted code.</span><span class='zh'>C++ Insights 将你的代码呈现为编译器的视角，揭示模板实例化与重载解析等后台过程。它帮助开发者理解意料之外的行为，并编写更健壮、抽象层次更清晰的代码。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A macOS project demonstrates on-device AI indexing for every photo and video frame, enabling fast, semantic search without cloud data.</span><span class='zh'>一个 macOS 项目展示了在本地对每张照片、每一帧视频进行 AI 索引，以实现快速的语义搜索。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A macOS project demonstrates on-device AI indexing for every photo and video frame, enabling fast, semantic search without cloud data. The approach emphasizes privacy and offline capabilities, showing potential for smarter photo management on desktops.</span><span class='zh'>一个 macOS 项目展示了在本地对每张照片、每一帧视频进行 AI 索引，以实现快速的语义搜索。该方法强调隐私与离线能力，给桌面端的照片管理带来更智能的可能性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://codeberg.org/shitter/shitter" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Shitter – A Nitter Fork</span><span class='zh'>Shitter：Nitter 的一个分叉实现</span></a><span class="dom">codeberg.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Shitter is a fork of Nitter, offering a privacy-conscious, JS-light front-end for Twitter-like content and a self-hosted viewing experience.</span><span class='zh'>Shitter 是 Nitter 的一个分叉，提供一个轻量、注重隐私的前端来浏览类似 Twitter 的内容，并支持自托管使用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Shitter is a fork of Nitter, offering a privacy-conscious, JS-light front-end for Twitter-like content and a self-hosted viewing experience. It exemplifies open-source efforts to reduce tracking and API reliance while preserving access to public posts.</span><span class='zh'>Shitter 是 Nitter 的一个分叉，提供一个轻量、注重隐私的前端来浏览类似 Twitter 的内容，并支持自托管使用。该项目展示了开源社区在减少跟踪、降低对官方 API 依赖方面的努力，同时保持对公开内容的访问。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/FrigadeHQ/hob" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Hob - Open-source, on device alternative to Claude Cowork and ChatGPT</span><span class='zh'>Hob：开源的本地替代，面向 Claude Cowork 与 ChatGPT</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hob enables an on-device AI experience, offering an open-source alternative to Claude Cowork and ChatGPT that runs entirely locally.</span><span class='zh'>Hob 开源本地化 AI 体验，提供面向 Claude Cowork 与 ChatGPT 的本地替代方案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hob enables an on-device AI experience, offering an open-source alternative to Claude Cowork and ChatGPT that runs entirely locally. It demonstrates the feasibility of private, offline inference on consumer hardware, though model size and compute demands remain practical constraints.</span><span class='zh'>Hob 开源本地化 AI 体验，提供面向 Claude Cowork 与 ChatGPT 的本地替代方案。它展示了在消费级硬件上实现私有、离线推理的可行性，但模型规模与计算需求仍是实际制约。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/PowderworksCode/headstart" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Emitting metadata early makes building/checking Rust up to twice as fast</span><span class='zh'>提前输出元数据使 Rust 构建与检查速度提升至两倍</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Emitting metadata early reduces repeated work during compilation, speeding up the development loop.</span><span class='zh'>提前输出元数据显著减少编译过程中的重复工作，加速增量构建并提升反馈速度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Emitting metadata early reduces repeated work during compilation, speeding up the development loop. This approach can halve build and check times, sharpening feedback for changes and guiding compiler design.</span><span class='zh'>提前输出元数据显著减少编译过程中的重复工作，加速增量构建并提升反馈速度。此举对构建系统和编译器设计也有启示。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/otya128/winevdm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>16-bit Windows (Windows 1.x, 2.x, 3.0, 3.1, etc.) on 64-bit Windows</span><span class='zh'>在64位 Windows 上运行16位 Windows 应用（1.x/2.x/3.0/3.1 等）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WineVDM provides a pathway to run 16-bit Windows apps (Windows 1.x, 2.x, 3.0/3.1) on 64-bit Windows by using a dedicated compatibility runtime.</span><span class='zh'>该项目通过 winevdm 在64位 Windows 上实现对16位 Windows 应用（1.x、2.x、3.0、3.1 等）的运行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WineVDM provides a pathway to run 16-bit Windows apps (Windows 1.x, 2.x, 3.0/3.1) on 64-bit Windows by using a dedicated compatibility runtime. It illustrates how modern Windows still lacks native 16-bit support, leaving preservation-focused projects to recreate the old environment. This matters for running legacy software, archaeology of software, and niche users who still rely on early Windows programs.</span><span class='zh'>该项目通过 winevdm 在64位 Windows 上实现对16位 Windows 应用（1.x、2.x、3.0、3.1 等）的运行。与现代 Windows 不再原生支持16位应用不同，兼容层/运行时的实现让老旧软件得以保存与使用，但仍存在兼容性与性能限制。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/27/top_stories_10272026'><span class='en'>Oct 27</span><span class='zh'>10月27日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>131</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50253529' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>31</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50038623" data-id="50038623" data-tags="Hardware|Gaming|Code" data-score="130" data-comments="13" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50038623" data-time="1791679549">

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PSPi 6 integrates a Raspberry Pi inside a PSP shell, enabling portable retro computing with modern hardware. Takeaway: it shows how small single-board computers can breathe new life into classic devices.</span><span class='zh'>PSPi 第六版将树莓派嵌入 PSP 机壳，实现带有现代硬件的便携式复古计算体验。该项目展示了巧妙的硬件黑客技巧，并降低了自行打造掌机的门槛。要点在于小型 SBC 能为经典设备注入新生命。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/bherbruck/solidcraft" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: SolidCraft – Open-Source Parametric CAD in Rust (With MCP Support)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：SolidCraft——用 Rust 编写的开源参数化 CAD（含 MCP 支持）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>SolidCraft is an open-source, Rust-based parametric CAD project with MCP support, enabling scriptable, reproducible design workflows.</span><span class='zh'>SolidCraft 是一个用 Rust 编写的开源参数化 CAD 项目，且支持 MCP，便于实现可脚本化、可重复的设计工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>SolidCraft is an open-source, Rust-based parametric CAD project with MCP support, enabling scriptable, reproducible design workflows. By leveraging Rust’s performance and safety, it targets developers who want fast, reliable CAD tooling without relying on heavy C++ stacks. The project signals growing interest in modern, open tooling for mechanical design.</span><span class='zh'>SolidCraft 是一个用 Rust 编写的开源参数化 CAD 项目，且支持 MCP，便于实现可脚本化、可重复的设计工作流。利用 Rust 的性能与安全性，它面向需要快速、可靠 CAD 工具的开发者。该项目反映了对现代、开源机械设计工具日益增长的需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/blog/nvidia/nemotron-transcribe-granary" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe: open ASR for 40 languages</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe：40 种语言的开源 ASR</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe brings open-source ASR to 40 languages, lowering the barrier for multilingual speech applications.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe 为40种语言提供开源自动语音识别，降低多语言语音应用的门槛。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe brings open-source ASR to 40 languages, lowering the barrier for multilingual speech applications. The release lets researchers compare performance against other open models and accelerates progress in open speech AI.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe 为40种语言提供开源自动语音识别，降低多语言语音应用的门槛。该发布使研究者能够与其它开源模型进行对比，加速开放语音 AI 的进展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Queuingtheorydotcom/11SquaresFormalized" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI-assisted proof of optimal packing for 11 squares</span><span class='zh'>AI 助力的11个正方形最优铺放证明</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An AI-assisted formal proof establishes the optimal packing of 11 equal squares, showcasing how AI search and formal verification can tackle challenging geometry problems.</span><span class='zh'>AI 辅助的严格证明确定了11个相等正方形的最优铺放方案，展示了 AI 搜索与形式化验证结合解决几何难题的潜力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An AI-assisted formal proof establishes the optimal packing of 11 equal squares, showcasing how AI search and formal verification can tackle challenging geometry problems. The work demonstrates the potential of hybrid human-AI reasoning in mathematics.</span><span class='zh'>AI 辅助的严格证明确定了11个相等正方形的最优铺放方案，展示了 AI 搜索与形式化验证结合解决几何难题的潜力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/SaarthurR/atticus-mac" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Atticus – Hold-to-talk voice assistant for macOS that runs on Claude -p</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Atticus——在 macOS 上运行的按住说话的语音助手，基于 Claude-p</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Atticus is an open-source macOS voice assistant that uses Claude with a &#x27;hold-to-talk&#x27; interface, enabling convenient, hands-free interaction.</span><span class='zh'>Atticus 是一个开源的 macOS 语音助手，采用 Claude-p 提供后台服务并使用“按住说话”的交互模式，便捷直观。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Atticus is an open-source macOS voice assistant that uses Claude with a &#x27;hold-to-talk&#x27; interface, enabling convenient, hands-free interaction. It demonstrates modular, privacy-friendly AI tooling on the desktop with a popular LLM backend.</span><span class='zh'>Atticus 是一个开源的 macOS 语音助手，采用 Claude-p 提供后台服务并使用“按住说话”的交互模式，便捷直观。展示了在桌面端使用主流大语言模型后端的模块化、注重隐私的 AI 工具链。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/kerlenton/bootgpt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">92</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kerlenton/bootgpt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A language model that lives in a boot sector</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：运行在引导扇区中的语言模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>BootGPT places an entire inference engine in a 510-byte MBR, with model weights, vocabulary, and strings stored in the following disk sectors.</span><span class='zh'>BootGPT 将完整推理引擎放入 510 字节的 MBR 中，并把模型权重、词汇表和字符串存储在后续磁盘扇区。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>BootGPT places an entire inference engine in a 510-byte MBR, with model weights, vocabulary, and strings stored in the following disk sectors. Its small character-level neural network was trained to generate Linux boot messages indefinitely, matching a simulator character by character. Debugging uncovered an x86 assembly bug involving the remainder left in EDX by integer division and a pointer that also used EDX.</span><span class='zh'>BootGPT 将完整推理引擎放入 510 字节的 MBR 中，并把模型权重、词汇表和字符串存储在后续磁盘扇区。这个小型字符级神经网络经过训练，可以无限生成 Linux 启动消息，并与模拟器逐字符匹配。调试过程中发现了一个 x86 汇编错误：整数除法会把余数留在 EDX，而偏置表指针也使用了 EDX。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>105</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50315782' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>6</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50160877" data-id="50160877" data-tags="Security|Programming|Code" data-score="102" data-comments="41" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50160877" data-time="1792465507">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/golang/go/issues/82345" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">93</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/golang/go/issues/82345" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Go will reject certificate chains containing eight distrusted SPKIs</span><span class='zh'>Go 将拒绝包含八个不可信 SPKI 的证书链</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Go is tightening TLS validation by rejecting certificate chains that include eight distrusted SPKI entries.</span><span class='zh'>Go 将通过在 TLS 验证中拒绝包含八个不可信 SPKI 的证书链，来收紧安全性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Go is tightening TLS validation by rejecting certificate chains that include eight distrusted SPKI entries. The change reduces attack surface from compromised keys and misissued certificates, nudging developers to audit complex chains or intermediates.</span><span class='zh'>Go 将通过在 TLS 验证中拒绝包含八个不可信 SPKI 的证书链，来收紧安全性。此举降低了被入侵密钥和被篡改证书带来的风险，促使开发者审查包含多中间证书的证书链。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/19/top_stories_10192026'><span class='en'>Oct 19</span><span class='zh'>10月19日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>102</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50160877' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>41</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50297697" data-id="50297697" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Code" data-score="99" data-comments="31" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50297697" data-time="1793316346">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mottopanikeiku/doomformer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I Turned DOOM into a Transformer</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：把 DOOM 变成了 Transformer</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Using DOOM as a testbed, the project applies Transformer-based models to game data, demonstrating how sequence models can learn planning or perception tasks in a classic game.</span><span class='zh'>在 DOOM 的数据上应用 Transformer，展示序列模型在经典游戏中的学习能力，涉及规划与感知任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Using DOOM as a testbed, the project applies Transformer-based models to game data, demonstrating how sequence models can learn planning or perception tasks in a classic game. It’s a compact, proof-of-concept that sparks ideas about cross-domain AI experiments.</span><span class='zh'>在 DOOM 的数据上应用 Transformer，展示序列模型在经典游戏中的学习能力，涉及规划与感知任务。这个小型的实现是跨领域 AI 实验的一个有趣原型，值得关注。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">95</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/math/tree/main/preprints/Integer-multiplication-below-n-log-n-September-23-2026" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Integer multiplication below n log n</span><span class='zh'>低于 n log n 的整数乘法</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A theoretical preprint claims a method for integer multiplication that beats the classic n log n bound, hinting at new algorithmic ideas in number theory.</span><span class='zh'>理论预印本提出在某些情形下的整数乘法复杂度可低于经典的 n log n 上界，揭示了新的算法思路。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A theoretical preprint claims a method for integer multiplication that beats the classic n log n bound, hinting at new algorithmic ideas in number theory. If validated, it could influence cryptography and numeric algorithms.</span><span class='zh'>理论预印本提出在某些情形下的整数乘法复杂度可低于经典的 n log n 上界，揭示了新的算法思路。若被验证，其对密码学与数值算法或有影响。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/Geramy/LemonSeed-Studio" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#f59e0b' data-tag='Hardware'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">96</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Geramy/LemonSeed-Studio" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Running the unmodified Linux amdgpu driver on an iPad</span><span class='zh'>在 iPad 上运行未修改的 Linux amdgpu 驱动</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An experimental project demonstrates loading the unmodified amdgpu driver on iPad hardware, highlighting the gaps between Linux driver support and consumer devices.</span><span class='zh'>一项实验性尝试在 iPad 硬件上运行未修改的 amdgpu 驱动，揭示了 Linux 驱动与消费级设备之间的差距。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An experimental project demonstrates loading the unmodified amdgpu driver on iPad hardware, highlighting the gaps between Linux driver support and consumer devices. It underlines the significant hurdles in achieving functional GPU acceleration and full system usability on non-traditional hardware.</span><span class='zh'>一项实验性尝试在 iPad 硬件上运行未修改的 amdgpu 驱动，揭示了 Linux 驱动与消费级设备之间的差距。强调在非传统硬件上实现显卡加速与完整系统可用性所面临的重大挑战。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/owls-on-wires/keplearn" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">97</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/owls-on-wires/keplearn" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Keplearn: Extract Equations from Data in Seconds</span><span class='zh'>Keplearn：秒级从数据中提取方程</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Keplearn is a tool that infers equations from data in seconds, enabling rapid symbolic modeling of complex systems.</span><span class='zh'>Keplearn 能在秒级从数据中推断出方程，快速实现对复杂系统的符号建模。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Keplearn is a tool that infers equations from data in seconds, enabling rapid symbolic modeling of complex systems. This accelerates scientific experimentation and can complement traditional regression with interpretable models.</span><span class='zh'>Keplearn 能在秒级从数据中推断出方程，快速实现对复杂系统的符号建模。这加速了科学实验，并可与传统回归方法结合，产生可解释的模型。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/29/top_stories_10292026'><span class='en'>Oct 29</span><span class='zh'>10月29日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>90</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50289590' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>10</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50018239" data-id="50018239" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="88" data-comments="37" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50018239" data-time="1791538986">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/alex0ptr/once" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/alex0ptr/once" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Once: Cache CLI commands</span><span class='zh'>Once：缓存 CLI 命令</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Once is a CLI utility that caches your frequently used shell commands for quick reuse.</span><span class='zh'>Once 是一个命令行工具，用于缓存常用命令以便快速重用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Once is a CLI utility that caches your frequently used shell commands for quick reuse. It speeds up repetitive tasks and improves reproducibility across sessions and machines. The tool targets developers and sysadmins who want smoother, repeatable workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Once 是一个命令行工具，用于缓存常用命令以便快速重用。它可加速重复任务并提升跨会话、跨机器的可重复性，适合开发者与系统管理员构建更顺畅的工作流程。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/09/top_stories_10092026'><span class='en'>Oct 9</span><span class='zh'>10月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>88</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50018239' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>37</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50175510" data-id="50175510" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="86" data-comments="31" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50175510" data-time="1792551227">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">99</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/zion-sati/NetWasm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>C# now beats Rust on WASM size</span><span class='zh'>C# 在 WASM 大小方面现在比 Rust 更小</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A WASM size comparison suggests C# compiled to WebAssembly can produce smaller binaries than Rust for certain workloads, challenging the assumption that Rust always yields the smallest output.</span><span class='zh'>对比显示在某些工作负载下，C# 编译为 WebAssembly 的二进制体积可能比 Rust 更小，挑战了“Rust 总是最小”的常识。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A WASM size comparison suggests C# compiled to WebAssembly can produce smaller binaries than Rust for certain workloads, challenging the assumption that Rust always yields the smallest output. This influences language choice for wasm-based apps, though ecosystem and performance considerations remain important.</span><span class='zh'>对比显示在某些工作负载下，C# 编译为 WebAssembly 的二进制体积可能比 Rust 更小，挑战了“Rust 总是最小”的常识。对 wasm 应用的语言选择产生影响，但生态和性能等因素仍然重要。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/21/top_stories_10212026'><span class='en'>Oct 21</span><span class='zh'>10月21日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>86</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50175510' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>31</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50005608" data-id="50005608" data-tags="AI|Code" data-score="86" data-comments="24" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50005608" data-time="1791466186">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/SamsungLabs/LittleBit" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">100</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/SamsungLabs/LittleBit" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sub-1-Bit LLM Compression via Latent Factorization</span><span class='zh'>通过潜在因子分解实现 Sub-1-Bit 的大语言模型压缩</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LittleBit uses latent factorization to compress large language models to sub-1-bit representations, aiming to retain useful inference quality at far smaller sizes.</span><span class='zh'>LittleBit 通过潜在因子分解把大模型压缩到低于1比特的表示，意在在显著减小模型规模的同时维持可用的推理质量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LittleBit uses latent factorization to compress large language models to sub-1-bit representations, aiming to retain useful inference quality at far smaller sizes. The approach could unlock running capable LLMs on edge devices and specialized accelerators, expanding deployment scenarios.</span><span class='zh'>LittleBit 通过潜在因子分解把大模型压缩到低于1比特的表示，意在在显著减小模型规模的同时维持可用的推理质量。该方法有望让边缘设备和定制加速器也能部署强大的 LLM，拓展应用场景。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/08/top_stories_10082026'><span class='en'>Oct 8</span><span class='zh'>10月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>86</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50005608' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>24</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50183892" data-id="50183892" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Open Source|Privacy|Code" data-score="85" data-comments="25" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50183892" data-time="1792601093">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/omnara-ai/omnara" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Omnara – Open-source alternative to Claude Managed Agents</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Omnara Open source 替代 Claude 管理代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Omnara is an open source alternative to Claude&#x27;s Managed Agents, offering configurable AI agents that you can run and customize yourself.</span><span class='zh'>Omnara 是一个开源替代方案，定位为可本地控管的 Claude Managed Agents 的对等实现，提供可配置的 AI 代理和可插拔集成。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Omnara is an open source alternative to Claude&#x27;s Managed Agents, offering configurable AI agents that you can run and customize yourself. The project emphasizes transparency and privacy and aims to match managed agent capabilities with self hosted deployment.</span><span class='zh'>Omnara 是一个开源替代方案，定位为可本地控管的 Claude Managed Agents 的对等实现，提供可配置的 AI 代理和可插拔集成。该项目强调透明度、可定制性和隐私，面向希望自托管 AI 自动化的团队。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/21/top_stories_10212026'><span class='en'>Oct 21</span><span class='zh'>10月21日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>85</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50183892' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>25</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50045273" data-id="50045273" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|Code" data-score="84" data-comments="25" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50045273" data-time="1791737902">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rogerbinns/apsw" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>apsw: Another Python SQLite Wrapper</span><span class='zh'>apsw：又一个 Python SQLite 封装器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>APSW offers a Python SQLite wrapper with lower-level access and extra features beyond the standard interface.</span><span class='zh'>APSW 是一个为 Python 提供 SQLite 封装的库，提供比内置 sqlite3 更低层次的访问和扩展功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>APSW offers a Python SQLite wrapper with lower-level access and extra features beyond the standard interface. It exposes advanced APIs for virtual tables, custom collations, and direct blob handling, appealing to power users who need fine-grained control—though it requires more maintenance.</span><span class='zh'>APSW 是一个为 Python 提供 SQLite 封装的库，提供比内置 sqlite3 更低层次的访问和扩展功能。它支持高级 API，如虚拟表、定制排序以及直接操作 BLOB，适合需要更细粒度控制的高级开发者；不过使用起来需要更多手工维护和编码约束。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/11/top_stories_10112026'><span class='en'>Oct 11</span><span class='zh'>10月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>84</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50045273' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>25</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50000676" data-id="50000676" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|AI|Code" data-score="84" data-comments="160" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50000676" data-time="1791420360">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/pingdotgg/ts-rust" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Port of the TypeScript compiler, checker and lsp to Rust, by LLM</span><span class='zh'>通过大模型驱动的 TypeScript 工具链移植到 Rust 的计划</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project porting the TypeScript toolchain to Rust with the help of a large language model, aiming for faster compilation and stronger safety guarantees.</span><span class='zh'>一个在大模型协助下将 TypeScript 编译器、检查器和语言服务器移植到 Rust 的尝试，目标是实现更快的编译与更高的安全性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project porting the TypeScript toolchain to Rust with the help of a large language model, aiming for faster compilation and stronger safety guarantees. It signals how LLMs might accelerate compiler tooling experiments.</span><span class='zh'>一个在大模型协助下将 TypeScript 编译器、检查器和语言服务器移植到 Rust 的尝试，目标是实现更快的编译与更高的安全性。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">104</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anubhavmalhotra/r2d2-tv" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Making a smart TV dumb and then smart</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：把智能电视变得更“傻”再重新变为智能</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A DIY project that strips embedded smart TV software to create a privacy-preserving, lean device, then reintroduces smart capabilities in a more controllable way.</span><span class='zh'>一个 DIY 项目通过移除智能电视的内置软件来打造一个轻量、注重隐私的设备，随后再以更可控的方式重新引入智能功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A DIY project that strips embedded smart TV software to create a privacy-preserving, lean device, then reintroduces smart capabilities in a more controllable way. The effort highlights the tension between vendor-bloat and user autonomy, and points to hackable hardware routes for better privacy.</span><span class='zh'>一个 DIY 项目通过移除智能电视的内置软件来打造一个轻量、注重隐私的设备，随后再以更可控的方式重新引入智能功能。此举凸显厂商臃肿软件与用户自主权之间的矛盾，并指向可 Hack 的硬件方式以提升隐私保护。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/23/top_stories_10232026'><span class='en'>Oct 23</span><span class='zh'>10月23日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>84</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202865' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>65</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49956003" data-id="49956003" data-tags="Security|Code" data-score="83" data-comments="12" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49956003" data-time="1791135534">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/net4people/bbs/issues/672" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Xray-core concealed a certificate verification bypass vulnerability</span><span class='zh'>Xray-core 隐藏的证书验证漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Xray-core contains a certificate verification bypass vulnerability that could allow man-in-the-middle attacks.</span><span class='zh'>Xray-core 存在一个证书验证绕过漏洞，可能被用来执行中间人攻击。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Xray-core contains a certificate verification bypass vulnerability that could allow man-in-the-middle attacks. The finding underscores the importance of verifying all certificate handling in proxy software and applying patches.</span><span class='zh'>Xray-core 存在一个证书验证绕过漏洞，可能被用来执行中间人攻击。这一发现强调了代理软件中对证书处理的严格验证和及时打补丁的重要性。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/04/top_stories_10042026'><span class='en'>Oct 4</span><span class='zh'>10月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>83</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49956003' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>12</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49953195" data-id="49953195" data-tags="Privacy|Open Source|Code" data-score="82" data-comments="10" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49953195" data-time="1791115750">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/dfd-tud/deda" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">106</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dfd-tud/deda" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DEDA – Tracking Dots Extraction, Decoding and Anonymisation Toolkit</span><span class='zh'>DEDA：跟踪点提取、解码与匿名化工具包</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DEDA provides a toolkit for extracting, decoding, and anonymizing tracking dots, enabling researchers to study marker-based tracking and improve privacy protections.</span><span class='zh'>DEDA 提供一个跟踪点提取、解码与匿名化的工具包，帮助研究者研究基于标记的跟踪并提升隐私保护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DEDA provides a toolkit for extracting, decoding, and anonymizing tracking dots, enabling researchers to study marker-based tracking and improve privacy protections. It highlights practical methods for analyzing and mitigating telemetry signals.</span><span class='zh'>DEDA 提供一个跟踪点提取、解码与匿名化的工具包，帮助研究者研究基于标记的跟踪并提升隐私保护。它展示了分析与缓解遥测信号的实际方法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/theconsensuslabs/noalloc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>noalloc: Tooling and libraries to enforce zero heap allocations in Golang</span><span class='zh'>noalloc：在 Golang 中实现零堆分配的工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Noalloc is a toolkit to enforce zero-heap allocations in Go programs, improving latency predictability for high-performance services.</span><span class='zh'>noalloc 提供工具与库，用于在 Go 程序中强制零堆分配，从而提升高性能服务的延迟可预测性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Noalloc is a toolkit to enforce zero-heap allocations in Go programs, improving latency predictability for high-performance services. It helps developers reason about memory use, though it may constrain certain idioms and require careful design.</span><span class='zh'>noalloc 提供工具与库，用于在 Go 程序中强制零堆分配，从而提升高性能服务的延迟可预测性。它帮助开发者管理内存使用，但可能限制某些编码习惯并需谨慎设计。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/emmilco/us-tax-model" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A proof-of-concept formalization of the IRS federal tax forms</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：将 IRS 税表形式化的概念性实现</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Show HN project formalizes IRS tax forms as a proof-of-concept, likely using a theorem prover or formal spec; demonstrates how to model tax forms for correctness and tooling; significance for software that handles regulated forms.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了将 IRS 税表形式化的概念性实现，借助形式化工具提高正确性与可验证性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Show HN project formalizes IRS tax forms as a proof-of-concept, likely using a theorem prover or formal spec; demonstrates how to model tax forms for correctness and tooling; significance for software that handles regulated forms. 2-3 sentences.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了将 IRS 税表形式化的概念性实现，借助形式化工具提高正确性与可验证性。此举对政府表单数字化和软件可靠性具有示范价值。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/explaindb/explaindb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ExplainDB: A Database System Built for Understandability</span><span class='zh'>ExplainDB：为可理解性而生的数据库系统</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ExplainDB is a database system designed with understandability in mind, likely offering transparent query planning and explainability features to help developers and operators reason about data flows.</span><span class='zh'>ExplainDB 是以可理解性为设计目标的数据库系统，可能提供透明的查询规划和可解释性特性，帮助开发者与运维更清晰地理解数据流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ExplainDB is a database system designed with understandability in mind, likely offering transparent query planning and explainability features to help developers and operators reason about data flows.</span><span class='zh'>ExplainDB 是以可理解性为设计目标的数据库系统，可能提供透明的查询规划和可解释性特性，帮助开发者与运维更清晰地理解数据流。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/astroautomata/PySR" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Symbolic Learning in Python and Julia</span><span class='zh'>Python 与 Julia 中的符号学习</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PySR blends symbolic regression with modern ML ecosystems to yield interpretable models in Python and Julia.</span><span class='zh'>PySR 将符号回归与现代机器学习生态系统相结合，在 Python 与 Julia 中生成可解释的模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PySR blends symbolic regression with modern ML ecosystems to yield interpretable models in Python and Julia. By combining mathematical discovery with scalable tooling, it helps uncover human-readable expressions that fit data.</span><span class='zh'>PySR 将符号回归与现代机器学习生态系统相结合，在 Python 与 Julia 中生成可解释的模型。通过将数学发现与可扩展工具链结合，它有助于发现能拟合数据且易读的表达式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/johnrash/bebox" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>BeBox is a Haiku application that emulates Be Inc.&#x27;s BeBox</span><span class='zh'>BeBox：在 Haiku 上模拟 BeInc 的 BeBox</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>BeBox is a Haiku app that emulates Be Inc.&#x27;s BeBox, allowing retro BeOS-era software to run on modern Haiku OS.</span><span class='zh'>BeBox 是一个在 Haiku 操作系统上运行的 BeBox 模拟应用，允许复古 BeOS 时代的软件在新环境中运行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>BeBox is a Haiku app that emulates Be Inc.&#x27;s BeBox, allowing retro BeOS-era software to run on modern Haiku OS. It&#x27;s a niche project that showcases emulation fidelity and the continued appeal of vintage systems. For enthusiasts, it&#x27;s a glimpse into how hobbyists keep old ecosystems alive.</span><span class='zh'>BeBox 是一个在 Haiku 操作系统上运行的 BeBox 模拟应用，允许复古 BeOS 时代的软件在新环境中运行。该项目展示了仿真与系统级再现的潜力，也反映出爱好者对旧系统的持续热情。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/p10node/k10s" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: K10s – A Clickable Kubernetes TUI (Go, Bubble Tea)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：K10s——一个可点击的 Kubernetes TUI（Go、Bubble Tea）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>K10s is a clickable terminal UI for Kubernetes built with Go and Bubble Tea, enabling point-and-click operations inside a TUI instead of pure command lines.</span><span class='zh'>K10s 是一个用 Go 和 Bubble Tea 构建的可点击 Kubernetes 终端界面，允许在 TUI 内进行点选操作而非仅用命令行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>K10s is a clickable terminal UI for Kubernetes built with Go and Bubble Tea, enabling point-and-click operations inside a TUI instead of pure command lines. By combining a familiar CLI domain with a UI that can be navigated via keyboard or mouse, it aims to reduce the cognitive load of managing clusters in the terminal. This project highlights how terminal interfaces can augment traditional DevOps tooling without sacrificing portability.</span><span class='zh'>K10s 是一个用 Go 和 Bubble Tea 构建的可点击 Kubernetes 终端界面，允许在 TUI 内进行点选操作而非仅用命令行。它将熟悉的命令行领域与可键盘/鼠标导航的 UI 结合，旨在降低终端管理集群的认知负担。该项目展示了终端工具如何在不牺牲可移植性的前提下变得更易用。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/08/top_stories_10082026'><span class='en'>Oct 8</span><span class='zh'>10月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>65</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50009904' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>49</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49935097" data-id="49935097" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|Hardware|Code" data-score="64" data-comments="10" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49935097" data-time="1790957088">

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>gpuvis turns GPU trace logs into interactive timelines, helping developers pinpoint where rendering or compute bottlenecks occur.</span><span class='zh'>gpuvis 将 GPU 跟踪日志转化为交互式时间线，帮助开发者定位渲染或计算瓶颈。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>gpuvis turns GPU trace logs into interactive timelines, helping developers pinpoint where rendering or compute bottlenecks occur. By visualizing GPU activity, it simplifies performance tuning across graphics pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>gpuvis 将 GPU 跟踪日志转化为交互式时间线，帮助开发者定位渲染或计算瓶颈。通过直观的可视化，简化了图形管线的性能调优。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kividbio/kividb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: KiviDB, a faster Redis-compatible memory database and vector cache</span><span class='zh'>KiviDB，一种更快的 Redis 兼容内存数据库与向量缓存</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>KiviDB positions as a faster Redis-compatible in-memory database with vector caching, targeting real-time analytics and AI workloads that demand low latency.</span><span class='zh'>KiviDB 被定位为更快的 Redis 兼容内存数据库，提供向量缓存，面向对延迟要求极高的实时分析与 AI 工作负载。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>KiviDB positions as a faster Redis-compatible in-memory database with vector caching, targeting real-time analytics and AI workloads that demand low latency.</span><span class='zh'>KiviDB 被定位为更快的 Redis 兼容内存数据库，提供向量缓存，面向对延迟要求极高的实时分析与 AI 工作负载。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/bealesh/neckbeard" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Neckbeard – A DevOps/platform/SRE plugin for your agent</span><span class='zh'>Neckbeard——为你的代理提供的 DevOps/平台/SRE 插件</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Neckbeard is a plugin collection that integrates agent workflows with DevOps, platform, and SRE tasks—think CI/CD interactions, infrastructure control, and observability hooks for agent-driven automation.</span><span class='zh'>Neckbeard 是一组插件，旨在将代理工作流与 DevOps、平台和 SRE 任务集成，包括 CI/CD、基础设施控制和可观测性接口。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Neckbeard is a plugin collection that integrates agent workflows with DevOps, platform, and SRE tasks—think CI/CD interactions, infrastructure control, and observability hooks for agent-driven automation. It aims to make agents more useful and safer in operational contexts by providing ready-made connectors and operational primitives.</span><span class='zh'>Neckbeard 是一组插件，旨在将代理工作流与 DevOps、平台和 SRE 任务集成，包括 CI/CD、基础设施控制和可观测性接口。它通过提供现成的连接器和操作原语，使代理在运行时管理场景中更实用且更安全。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/14/top_stories_10142026'><span class='en'>Oct 14</span><span class='zh'>10月14日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>59</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50091794' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>49</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50006930" data-id="50006930" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Code" data-score="55" data-comments="8" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50006930" data-time="1791472999">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/moritzwilksch/diffle" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Diffle, a keyboard-centric diff viewer with LSP support</span><span class='zh'>Diffle：以键盘为中心的 Diff 查看器，支持 LSP</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Diffle is a diff viewer designed for keyboard-first navigation with added LSP support for code-aware diffs.</span><span class='zh'>Diffle 是一个以键盘优先为设计的差异查看工具，并集成了对 LSP 的支持，提升对 diffs 的代码感知。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Diffle is a diff viewer designed for keyboard-first navigation with added LSP support for code-aware diffs. It aims to speed up reviews and patch creation by minimizing mouse use and providing inline intelligence. Takeaway: keyboard-centric tools with code intelligence can accelerate code comparison workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Diffle 是一个以键盘优先为设计的差异查看工具，并集成了对 LSP 的支持，提升对 diffs 的代码感知。通过最小化鼠标使用并提供代码智能，能加速审查和补丁编写。要点是键盘优先的工具结合代码智能能提升效率。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/arterialist/kube32" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">133</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/arterialist/kube32" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I rebuilt Kubernetes for ESP32 microcontrollers</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我为 ESP32 微控制器重做了 Kubernetes</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kube32 is a Kubernetes-compatible control plane and WebAssembly pod runtime for ESP32-S3 microcontrollers.</span><span class='zh'>Kube32 是面向 ESP32-S3 微控制器的 Kubernetes 兼容控制平面和 WebAssembly Pod 运行时。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kube32 is a Kubernetes-compatible control plane and WebAssembly pod runtime for ESP32-S3 microcontrollers. The project lets kubectl and k9s manage boards over Wi-Fi or USB serial, while pods run WebAssembly modules through the wasm3 interpreter. A board with PSRAM serves as the control plane, and boards without PSRAM act as workers. The same sources also build a macOS and Linux host simulator.</span><span class='zh'>Kube32 是面向 ESP32-S3 微控制器的 Kubernetes 兼容控制平面和 WebAssembly Pod 运行时。该项目支持通过 Wi-Fi 或 USB 串口使用 kubectl 和 k9s 管理开发板，并通过 wasm3 解释器运行 WebAssembly 模块。带 PSRAM 的开发板充当控制平面，不带 PSRAM 的开发板作为工作节点。同一套源代码还可构建 macOS 和 Linux 主机模拟器。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>50</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50291812' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>16</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49991986" data-id="49991986" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Code" data-score="48" data-comments="13" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49991986" data-time="1791377106">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">134</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/akawashiro/procinsh" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Procinsh – A 3D Linux process inspector</span><span class='zh'>Procinsh：一个3D Linux 进程检查工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Show HN: Procinsh – A 3D Linux process inspector</span><span class='zh'>Procinsh 是一个3D Linux 进程检查工具，提供运行中的进程可视化以便调试和性能分析。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Show HN: Procinsh – A 3D Linux process inspector</span><span class='zh'>Procinsh 是一个3D Linux 进程检查工具，提供运行中的进程可视化以便调试和性能分析。</span></p>
